## Tomato - Viral Disease (Tomato Spotted Wilt Virus) / 番茄 - 病毒病

[Tomato | Viral Disease] Q: What causes this?
A: Tomato spotted wilt is caused by Tomato Spotted Wilt Virus (TSWV), a tospovirus transmitted almost entirely by thrips, especially western flower thrips. Only immature thrips can acquire the virus, but the adults they grow into carry and spread it for the rest of their lives, so an early thrips infestation is what actually introduces the disease. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[番茄 | 病毒病] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄斑萎病由番茄斑萎病毒（TSWV）引起，主要通过蓟马传播，尤其是西花蓟马。只有蓟马幼虫期才能获得病毒，但它们长为成虫后会终生携带并传播病毒，因此早期的蓟马侵染是该病爆发的关键原因。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Tomato | Viral Disease] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for wilting combined with leaf curling and a purplish or bronze discoloration on young leaves. As the disease progresses, ring-shaped spots may appear on fruit, and plant growth becomes stunted and uneven. These symptoms together (not any single one) are what distinguish TSWV from ordinary wilting. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[番茄 | 病毒病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：注意萎蔫并伴随叶片卷曲，幼叶出现紫色或古铜色变色。病情发展后，果实上可能出现环状斑点，植株生长会变得矮小且不均匀。这些症状同时出现（而非单一症状）才是判断TSWV病毒病的关键依据。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Tomato | Viral Disease] Q: How can I manage it?
A: There is no cure once a plant is infected, so management focuses on prevention: avoid planting near onion, garlic, or cereal fields since thrips build up heavily on them, use yellow sticky traps to monitor thrips populations, and remove and destroy infected plants immediately (rogue them out) to stop the virus from spreading to healthy plants nearby. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[番茄 | 病毒病] 问：如何防治？
答：植株一旦感染便无法治愈，因此防治重点在于预防：避免在洋葱、大蒜或谷类作物附近种植，因为蓟马容易在这些作物上大量繁殖；使用黄色粘虫板监测蓟马种群；发现感病植株应立即拔除销毁，以防病毒传播给周围健康植株。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Tomato | Viral Disease] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is a warm-season fruiting vegetable in the nightshade family, grown widely for fresh consumption and processing. It is highly sensitive to insect-vectored viruses, which is why thrips and aphid control is a routine part of tomato pest management programs, separate from managing fungal or bacterial diseases. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[番茄 | 病毒病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄是茄科的一种喜温果菜，广泛用于鲜食和加工。番茄对昆虫传播的病毒病非常敏感，因此蓟马和蚜虫的防治是番茄病虫害管理中的常规工作，这与防治真菌或细菌性病害是分开进行的。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Tomato - Blight / 番茄 - 疫病

[Tomato | Blight] Q: What causes this?
A: Blight in tomato (疫病) is commonly caused by Phytophthora species, water-mold pathogens that thrive in wet, poorly-drained soil and spread rapidly during periods of rain or overhead irrigation. Infection usually starts at the stem base or roots and moves upward through the plant. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[番茄 | 疫病] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄疫病通常由疫霉菌（Phytophthora）引起，这是一种在潮湿、排水不良的土壤中容易滋生的病原菌，在多雨或喷灌频繁的条件下传播迅速。感染通常从茎基部或根部开始，逐渐向上蔓延。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Tomato | Blight] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Early signs include dark, water-soaked lesions on stems near the soil line and sudden wilting of the whole plant even though the soil is moist. As the disease advances, the stem base becomes soft and discolored, and the plant can collapse within days under warm, wet conditions. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[番茄 | 疫病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：早期症状包括近地面茎部出现暗色水浸状病斑，即使土壤湿润植株也会突然萎蔫。病情加重后，茎基部变软并变色，在温暖潮湿条件下植株可能在几天内整株倒伏。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Tomato | Blight] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Improve drainage and avoid overhead irrigation to reduce the wet conditions the pathogen needs, rotate away from tomato and other nightshades for at least two to three years in affected fields, and remove infected plants promptly since the pathogen survives in soil and plant debris. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[番茄 | 疫病] 问：如何防治？
答：改善田间排水，避免喷灌，以减少病原菌所需的潮湿环境；受害地块应与番茄及其他茄科作物轮作至少两到三年；及时清除病株，因为病原菌可在土壤和植株残体中长期存活。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Tomato | Blight] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato performs best in well-drained soil with consistent but not excessive moisture. Because several of its most serious diseases, including blight, are driven by wet conditions at the root zone, drainage and irrigation management are as important to tomato health as pest control. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[番茄 | 疫病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄在排水良好、水分适中但不过湿的土壤中长势最佳。由于包括疫病在内的多种严重病害都与根部积水有关，因此排水和灌溉管理对番茄的健康与虫害防治同样重要。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Tomato - Bacterial Disease / 番茄 - 细菌性

[Tomato | Bacterial Disease] Q: What causes this?
A: Bacterial spot on tomato is caused by Xanthomonas bacteria, which are seed-borne and can also survive on infected transplants without showing symptoms until conditions favor an outbreak. Warm temperatures above 20°C combined with high humidity or splashing water (rain or overhead irrigation) are what trigger visible disease. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[番茄 | 细菌性] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄细菌性斑点病由黄单胞菌（Xanthomonas）引起，该病原可通过种子传播，也能在感病但未表现症状的种苗上潜伏，直到条件适宜时才爆发。20摄氏度以上的温度配合高湿度或雨水、喷灌溅水，是诱发明显病害的主要条件。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Tomato | Bacterial Disease] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for small, dark, water-soaked spots on leaves that turn brown to black and feel slightly raised; leaf spot centers often dry out and tear, giving leaves a ragged look. On fruit, spots start small and scabby and can enlarge and merge as the disease progresses. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[番茄 | 细菌性] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：注意叶片上出现细小、暗色、水浸状斑点，随后变为褐色至黑色并略微隆起；病斑中心常常干裂，使叶片呈现破损状。果实上的病斑起初细小粗糙，病情加重后会扩大并连合。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Tomato | Bacterial Disease] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use pathogen-free seed and disease-free transplants whenever possible, since this is the single most effective way to prevent an outbreak. Preventive copper sprays combined with these cultural practices help manage the disease once conditions become favorable, and avoiding overhead watering reduces bacterial spread between plants. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[番茄 | 细菌性] 问：如何防治？
答：尽可能使用无病原种子和健康种苗，这是预防该病最有效的方法。在条件适宜发病时，配合预防性喷施铜制剂可帮助控制病情；避免喷灌浇水可减少细菌在植株间的传播。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Tomato | Bacterial Disease] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is grown in a wide range of climates but is particularly vulnerable to bacterial diseases during warm, humid growing seasons. Because the bacteria that cause spot diseases can hide symptomlessly in transplants, sourcing clean seedlings is one of the most important tomato-growing decisions a farmer makes. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[番茄 | 细菌性] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄适应多种气候种植，但在温暖潮湿的生长季节中尤其容易感染细菌性病害。由于引起斑点病的细菌可在种苗上无症状潜伏，因此选用干净健康的种苗是种植番茄时最重要的决定之一。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Tomato - Blossom end rot / 番茄 - 脐腐病

[Tomato | Blossom end rot] Q: What causes this?
A: Blossom end rot is a physiological disorder, not an infectious disease. It develops when rapidly growing fruit does not receive enough calcium, often because irregular soil moisture, root stress, high salts, or overly rapid growth disrupt calcium movement into the fruit. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[番茄 | 脐腐病] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄脐腐病是一种生理性障碍，并非传染性病害。快速发育的果实得不到足够钙时会发生，常与土壤水分忽干忽湿、根系受压、盐分过高或生长过快影响钙运输有关。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Tomato | Blossom end rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for a pale or water-soaked spot at the blossom end of young fruit. It enlarges and becomes brown to black, sunken, dry, and leathery; secondary rots may later invade the damaged tissue. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[番茄 | 脐腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：幼果花端先出现浅色或水渍状斑点，随后扩大并变成褐色至黑色、凹陷、干燥和革质状，受损组织之后可能继发腐烂。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Tomato | Blossom end rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Keep soil moisture even, mulch where appropriate, avoid root injury and excessive fertilization, and use a soil test before adding calcium. Pesticides do not cure blossom end rot, and damaged fruit will not recover. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[番茄 | 脐腐病] 问：如何防治？
答：保持土壤水分稳定，可适当覆盖地表；避免伤根和过量施肥。补钙前应先进行土壤检测。农药不能治愈脐腐病，已经受害的果实也不会恢复。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Tomato | Blossom end rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is a warm-season nightshade crop. Consistent irrigation, good air movement, clean planting material, and regular scouting for insects and leaf symptoms are central to reducing disease and pest pressure. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[番茄 | 脐腐病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄是喜温的茄科作物。保持稳定灌溉、良好通风、使用健康种苗，并定期检查虫害和叶片症状，是降低病虫害风险的关键。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Tomato - Early Blight / 番茄 - 早疫病

[Tomato | Early Blight] Q: What causes this?
A: Early blight is caused by Alternaria solani, which survives on infected crop residue and related weeds. Spores spread by wind and splashing water, and infection is favored by prolonged leaf wetness and humid weather. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[番茄 | 早疫病] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄早疫病主要由茄链格孢菌（Alternaria solani）引起，病原可在病残体和茄科杂草上存活，孢子通过风和溅水传播，叶片长时间潮湿和高湿有利于发病。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Tomato | Early Blight] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Older leaves usually develop brown to black spots with concentric rings, often described as target-like lesions. Severe infection causes yellowing and leaf loss; fruit lesions are typically dark, dry, sunken, and may also show rings. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[番茄 | 早疫病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：老叶通常先出现褐色至黑色病斑，常具有同心轮纹，呈靶纹状。严重时叶片黄化脱落，果实可出现暗色、干燥、凹陷并带轮纹的病斑。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Tomato | Early Blight] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Remove infected debris and volunteer nightshades, rotate away from tomato and potato, reduce prolonged leaf wetness, and use locally registered fungicides only when disease pressure and local guidance justify treatment. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[番茄 | 早疫病] 问：如何防治？
答：清除病残体和自生茄科植株，与番茄和马铃薯等作物轮作，减少叶片长时间潮湿；在病害压力较高且当地建议需要时，再使用登记允许的杀菌剂。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Tomato | Early Blight] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is a warm-season nightshade crop. Consistent irrigation, good air movement, clean planting material, and regular scouting for insects and leaf symptoms are central to reducing disease and pest pressure. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[番茄 | 早疫病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄是喜温的茄科作物。保持稳定灌溉、良好通风、使用健康种苗，并定期检查虫害和叶片症状，是降低病虫害风险的关键。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Tomato - Late Blight / 番茄 - 晚疫病

[Tomato | Late Blight] Q: What causes this?
A: Late blight is caused by Phytophthora infestans, an oomycete that spreads rapidly in cool, wet conditions through airborne spores and infected plant material. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[番茄 | 晚疫病] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄晚疫病由疫霉菌 Phytophthora infestans 引起，在凉爽潮湿条件下可通过空气传播的孢子和带病植株材料迅速扩散。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Tomato | Late Blight] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Leaves develop rapidly enlarging water-soaked or brown lesions, often with pale or whitish sporulation near lesion margins in humid weather. Stems can darken, and fruit may develop firm brown lesions. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[番茄 | 晚疫病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：叶片出现快速扩大的水渍状或褐色病斑，潮湿时病斑边缘可见浅色或白色霉状物；茎部可变暗，果实可出现坚实褐色病斑。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Tomato | Late Blight] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use healthy transplants, remove infected plants promptly, avoid leaving cull piles or infected volunteer plants, improve airflow, and follow regional late-blight alerts and registered fungicide guidance where available. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[番茄 | 晚疫病] 问：如何防治？
答：使用健康种苗，及时清除病株和病果堆，清除带病自生植株，改善通风，并根据当地晚疫病预警和登记用药指南进行防治。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Tomato | Late Blight] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is a warm-season nightshade crop. Consistent irrigation, good air movement, clean planting material, and regular scouting for insects and leaf symptoms are central to reducing disease and pest pressure. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[番茄 | 晚疫病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄是喜温的茄科作物。保持稳定灌溉、良好通风、使用健康种苗，并定期检查虫害和叶片症状，是降低病虫害风险的关键。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Tomato - Powdery Mildew / 番茄 - 白粉病

[Tomato | Powdery Mildew] Q: What causes this?
A: Tomato powdery mildew is caused by powdery mildew fungi that spread by airborne spores. Disease is often favored by moderate temperatures, dense canopies, and conditions that support fungal growth even without free water on leaves. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[番茄 | 白粉病] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄白粉病由白粉菌类真菌引起，孢子可随空气传播。适中温度、冠层过密等环境有利于病害发生，即使叶面没有明显积水也可发展。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Tomato | Powdery Mildew] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for white powdery patches on leaves that enlarge over time, with associated yellowing, browning, and premature leaf drop when disease becomes severe. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[番茄 | 白粉病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：叶片上出现白色粉状斑块并逐渐扩大，严重时伴随黄化、褐变和提前落叶。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Tomato | Powdery Mildew] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Improve plant spacing and airflow, remove heavily affected tissue where practical, avoid excessive nitrogen, and use resistant varieties or locally registered fungicides when available and justified. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[番茄 | 白粉病] 问：如何防治？
答：改善株距和通风，适当清除严重受害组织，避免氮肥过量；条件允许时选用抗病品种，并按当地建议使用登记杀菌剂。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Tomato | Powdery Mildew] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is a warm-season nightshade crop. Consistent irrigation, good air movement, clean planting material, and regular scouting for insects and leaf symptoms are central to reducing disease and pest pressure. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[番茄 | 白粉病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄是喜温的茄科作物。保持稳定灌溉、良好通风、使用健康种苗，并定期检查虫害和叶片症状，是降低病虫害风险的关键。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Tomato - Fusarium Wilt / 番茄 - 镰刀菌枯萎病

[Tomato | Fusarium Wilt] Q: What causes this?
A: Fusarium wilt is caused by soilborne Fusarium oxysporum strains that invade tomato roots and vascular tissue. The pathogen can persist in soil and is favored when susceptible plants are grown repeatedly in infested ground. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[番茄 | 镰刀菌枯萎病] 问：病因是什么？
答：番茄镰刀菌枯萎病由土传尖孢镰刀菌相关菌株引起，病原从根部侵入维管组织，可在土壤中长期存活，连续种植感病番茄会增加风险。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Tomato | Fusarium Wilt] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Lower leaves yellow and wilt first, often beginning on one side of the plant. Wilting progresses upward, growth is stunted, and cutting the lower stem may reveal brown vascular discoloration. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[番茄 | 镰刀菌枯萎病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：下部叶片先黄化和萎蔫，常先发生在植株一侧，随后向上发展，植株生长受抑；切开近地面茎部可见维管组织褐变。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Tomato | Fusarium Wilt] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use resistant cultivars and clean transplants, avoid moving contaminated soil, manage root stress, and rotate with nonhost crops where useful. Severely affected plants should be removed. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[番茄 | 镰刀菌枯萎病] 问：如何防治？
答：使用抗病品种和健康种苗，避免携带污染土壤，减少根系胁迫，并在条件适宜时与非寄主作物轮作。严重病株应及时清除。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Tomato | Fusarium Wilt] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is a warm-season nightshade crop. Consistent irrigation, good air movement, clean planting material, and regular scouting for insects and leaf symptoms are central to reducing disease and pest pressure. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[番茄 | 镰刀菌枯萎病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄是喜温的茄科作物。保持稳定灌溉、良好通风、使用健康种苗，并定期检查虫害和叶片症状，是降低病虫害风险的关键。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Tomato - Whiteflies / 番茄 - 白粉虱

[Tomato | Whiteflies] Q: What causes this?
A: Whitefly infestations occur when small sap-feeding whiteflies colonize leaf undersides. Warm conditions favor rapid population growth, and some whitefly species can also transmit plant viruses. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[番茄 | 白粉虱] 问：病因是什么？
答：白粉虱侵染是由成虫和若虫在叶片背面刺吸汁液造成的。温暖条件下种群增长迅速，部分白粉虱种类还可传播植物病毒。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Tomato | Whiteflies] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for tiny white adults that fly when leaves are disturbed, scale-like immature stages under leaves, sticky honeydew, sooty mold, yellowing, and reduced vigor. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[番茄 | 白粉虱] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：轻触叶片时可见小型白色成虫飞起，叶背有鳞片状若虫，并可能出现蜜露、煤污、叶片黄化和植株长势下降。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Tomato | Whiteflies] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Monitor leaf undersides and sticky traps, remove heavily infested plant material, control weeds that host whiteflies, conserve natural enemies, and use selective controls when thresholds justify treatment. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[番茄 | 白粉虱] 问：如何防治？
答：检查叶片背面并使用粘虫板监测，清除重度受害组织和寄主杂草，保护天敌，在虫口密度达到防治需要时优先采用选择性措施。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Tomato | Whiteflies] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is a warm-season nightshade crop. Consistent irrigation, good air movement, clean planting material, and regular scouting for insects and leaf symptoms are central to reducing disease and pest pressure. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[番茄 | 白粉虱] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄是喜温的茄科作物。保持稳定灌溉、良好通风、使用健康种苗，并定期检查虫害和叶片症状，是降低病虫害风险的关键。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

# ===================================================================
# PEPPER (CHILI) / 辣椒
# ===================================================================

## Pepper - Viral Disease / 辣椒 - 病毒病

[Pepper | Viral Disease] Q: What causes this?
A: The main viral threat to pepper is also Tomato Spotted Wilt Virus (TSWV), spread by thrips in exactly the same way as in tomato. Peppers grown near onion, garlic, cereal fields, or greenhouses growing cut flowers are at higher risk because these host thrips populations that carry the virus. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[辣椒 | 病毒病] 问：病因是什么？
答：辣椒的主要病毒威胁同样是番茄斑萎病毒（TSWV），其传播方式与在番茄上完全相同，均由蓟马传播。种植在洋葱、大蒜、谷类作物附近或切花温室附近的辣椒风险更高，因为这些地方容易滋生携带病毒的蓟马种群。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Pepper | Viral Disease] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Infected plants show distorted new growth, leaf mottling, and ring or line patterns on leaves and fruit. Growth is often stunted and uneven across the field rather than affecting every plant equally, which is a useful clue when distinguishing it from nutrient problems. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[辣椒 | 病毒病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：感病植株表现为新生组织畸形、叶片斑驳，叶片和果实上出现环状或条状斑纹。植株生长通常矮小且田间分布不均，并非所有植株均等受害，这是区分病毒病与营养问题的重要线索。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Pepper | Viral Disease] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Avoid planting pepper adjacent to onion, garlic, or cereal crops that harbor thrips, use reflective mulch early in the season to repel thrips before they settle, and deploy yellow sticky traps for ongoing monitoring. Rogue out and destroy infected plants as soon as they are found. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[辣椒 | 病毒病] 问：如何防治？
答：避免将辣椒种植在容易滋生蓟马的洋葱、大蒜或谷类作物旁边；生长季早期使用反光地膜驱避蓟马，防止其定殖；持续使用黄色粘虫板监测虫情。发现感病植株应立即拔除销毁。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Pepper | Viral Disease] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Pepper is a warm-season crop closely related to tomato and shares many of the same insect and virus pressures. Its fruit set and quality are especially sensitive to early-season stress, so controlling thrips before flowering matters more for pepper than for many other vegetables. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[辣椒 | 病毒病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：辣椒是一种喜温作物，与番茄近缘，面临许多相同的虫害和病毒压力。辣椒的坐果和品质对生长早期的胁迫尤为敏感，因此在开花前控制蓟马对辣椒比对许多其他蔬菜更为重要。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Pepper - Aphids / 辣椒 - 蚜虫

[Pepper | Aphids] Q: What causes this?
A: Aphid infestations occur when green peach aphids and other species colonize the undersides of leaves and new growth tips, reproducing rapidly in mild, dry weather. Aphids are also a common way viruses get introduced into a pepper planting, since they can pick up and transmit plant viruses as they feed. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[辣椒 | 蚜虫] 问：病因是什么？
答：蚜虫侵染是由于桃蚜等蚜虫种类在叶片背面和新梢上聚集繁殖，在温和干燥的天气条件下繁殖速度很快。蚜虫也是病毒传入辣椒地的常见途径，因为它们取食时可获取并传播植物病毒。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Pepper | Aphids] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for clusters of small, soft-bodied insects on the underside of leaves and along stems, shiny sticky honeydew on leaves, shed white skins from molting, curled or distorted new leaves, and the presence of ants attracted to the honeydew. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[辣椒 | 蚜虫] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：留意叶片背面和茎秆上成簇的小型软体昆虫、叶片上发亮的粘性蜜露、蜕皮留下的白色皮壳、卷曲或畸形的新叶，以及被蜜露吸引而来的蚂蚁。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Pepper | Aphids] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Monitor weekly by inspecting the underside of leaves and new growth directly, rather than relying only on sticky traps, since aphid identification at the species level matters for choosing the right biological control. Predatory lady beetles and aphid parasitic wasps are effective biological controls where infestations are moderate. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[辣椒 | 蚜虫] 问：如何防治？
答：每周直接检查叶片背面和新梢进行监测，不要仅依赖粘虫板，因为准确识别蚜虫种类对选择合适的生物防治方法很重要。在中等程度侵染下，瓢虫等捕食性天敌和蚜虫寄生蜂是有效的生物防治手段。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Pepper | Aphids] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Pepper produces new growth continuously through its fruiting season, and that tender new growth is exactly what aphids prefer to colonize. This makes aphid monitoring an ongoing task throughout the growing season rather than a one-time inspection. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[辣椒 | 蚜虫] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：辣椒在整个结果期会持续长出新梢，而这些嫩梢正是蚜虫最喜欢聚集的部位。因此蚜虫监测需要贯穿整个生长季持续进行，而非一次性检查。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Pepper - Thrips / 辣椒 - 蓟马

[Pepper | Thrips] Q: What causes this?
A: Thrips infest pepper directly for feeding and, more seriously, act as the vector that introduces Tomato Spotted Wilt Virus into the planting. The adults are the only life stage that flies, though poorly, and are mostly spread by wind currents and on clothing or plant material. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[辣椒 | 蓟马] 问：病因是什么？
答：蓟马直接取食辣椒造成危害，更严重的是它们是传播番茄斑萎病毒的媒介昆虫。成虫是唯一能飞行的虫态，但飞行能力较弱，主要通过风力以及附着在衣物或植株残体上传播扩散。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Pepper | Thrips] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Feeding damage distorts plant growth, deforms flowers, and leaves white-to-silvery patches on emerging leaves that often contain tiny black fecal specks. High populations cause visible silvering or scarring on leaf and fruit surfaces along with edema-like blemishes on fruit. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[辣椒 | 蓟马] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：取食为害会导致植株生长畸形、花朵变形，新叶上出现白色至银白色斑块，斑块中常伴有黑色小粪便点。高密度虫害会在叶片和果实表面造成明显的银斑或疤痕，果实上还可能出现水肿状斑痕。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Pepper | Thrips] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use yellow sticky traps to monitor thrips movement (the same traps also catch aphids and whiteflies), apply reflective mulch early in the season to repel incoming thrips, and avoid planting near onion, garlic, or cereal fields where thrips populations build up heavily. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[辣椒 | 蓟马] 问：如何防治？
答：使用黄色粘虫板监测蓟马活动情况（同一粘虫板也能诱集蚜虫和白粉虱）；生长季早期铺设反光地膜驱避迁入的蓟马；避免在容易大量滋生蓟马的洋葱、大蒜或谷类作物附近种植。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Pepper | Thrips] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Pepper is one of the key host crops for western flower thrips in field settings, alongside tomato and lettuce. Because thrips damage and virus transmission are closely linked in pepper, effective thrips management is really virus-prevention management as well. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[辣椒 | 蓟马] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：辣椒与番茄、莴苣一样，是西花蓟马在田间的主要寄主作物之一。由于辣椒上蓟马的为害与病毒传播密切相关，有效防治蓟马实际上也就是在进行病毒病的预防。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Pepper - Mites / 辣椒 - 螨

[Pepper | Mites] Q: What causes this?
A: Spider mite outbreaks on pepper are favored by hot, dry weather, which speeds up mite reproduction, and by the loss of natural predators, often from earlier broad-spectrum insecticide use. Mites are found mainly on the undersides of leaves, out of sight until damage is already visible from above. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[辣椒 | 螨] 问：病因是什么？
答：辣椒上红蜘蛛（叶螨）暴发的原因主要是炎热干燥的天气，这种气候会加速螨类繁殖；同时天敌的减少（通常由于此前使用了广谱杀虫剂）也是重要原因。螨类主要藏匿在叶片背面，往往等到叶面上已能看出受害症状时才被发现。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Pepper | Mites] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for tiny yellow or white stippling dots on the upper leaf surface, visible when viewing leaves from above, while the mites themselves are found on the underside. Heavy infestations produce fine webbing across leaves and can cause overall leaf bronzing or drop. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[辣椒 | 螨] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：留意叶片正面出现的细小黄白色斑点（俗称"白斑"），从上方观察叶片即可看到，而螨虫本身则藏在叶片背面。严重侵染时叶片上会出现细密的蛛网，并可能导致叶片整体呈古铜色或脱落。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Pepper | Mites] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Treat as soon as mites are found at any level, since populations multiply rapidly in hot, dry conditions and become much harder to control once webbing covers the plant. Miticides that are gentler on beneficial insects are preferred where predatory mites are still present in the field. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[辣椒 | 螨] 问：如何防治？
答：一旦发现螨虫，无论密度高低都应立即处理，因为在炎热干燥条件下种群会迅速倍增，一旦蛛网覆盖全株就更难控制。在田间仍有捕食性螨类存在的情况下，优先选用对天敌较温和的杀螨剂。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Pepper | Mites] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Pepper's broad leaf canopy and the tendency of mites to hide on leaf undersides mean visual scouting from above the canopy alone will miss early infestations. Turning leaves over during routine field checks is an important habit for pepper growers. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[辣椒 | 螨] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：辣椒叶片冠层较大，而螨虫又习惯藏匿在叶背，因此仅从植株上方观察容易错过早期虫害。种植者在日常田间巡查时养成翻看叶片背面的习惯十分重要。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Pepper - Anthracnose / 辣椒 - 炭疽病

[Pepper | Anthracnose] Q: What causes this?
A: Anthracnose on pepper is caused by Colletotrichum fungal species that infect immature fruit while it is still in the field, though visible symptoms often do not appear until after harvest. No anthracnose-resistant pepper cultivar has yet been developed, which is why this disease remains a persistent priority in pepper production. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[辣椒 | 炭疽病] 问：病因是什么？
答：辣椒炭疽病由炭疽菌（Colletotrichum）真菌引起，该病原在果实尚未成熟时就已在田间侵染，但症状往往要到采后才明显表现出来。目前尚未培育出抗炭疽病的辣椒品种，因此该病始终是辣椒生产中需要重点关注的问题。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Pepper | Anthracnose] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Early lesions are small and water-soaked, expanding rapidly into sunken spots that range from dark red to tan to black. As infection progresses, small buff or salmon-colored spore masses appear scattered or in concentric rings within the lesion. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[辣椒 | 炭疽病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：早期病斑细小、呈水浸状，会迅速扩大为凹陷斑，颜色从暗红色到黄褐色再到黑色不等。随着病情发展，病斑内会散生或呈同心圆状分布浅黄色至橙红色的孢子堆。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Pepper | Anthracnose] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Start with clean, pathogen-free seed and rotate crops away from pepper and related hosts to reduce carryover inoculum in the field. Preventive fungicide sprays timed to periods of favorable warm, humid weather provide additional protection where the disease has been a problem before. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[辣椒 | 炭疽病] 问：如何防治？
答：首先应使用无病原的洁净种子，并与辣椒及其近缘寄主作物轮作，以减少田间病原菌的残留积累。在此前曾发病的地块，可在温暖潮湿的天气条件到来前预防性喷施杀菌剂，以提供额外保护。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Pepper | Anthracnose] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Because anthracnose symptoms on pepper fruit frequently appear only after harvest, post-harvest handling and storage conditions matter almost as much as field management for controlling this particular disease. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[辣椒 | 炭疽病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：由于辣椒炭疽病的症状常常在采收后才显现，因此采后处理和贮藏条件对控制这种病害的重要性，几乎不亚于田间管理本身。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Pepper - Bacterial Spot / 辣椒 - 细菌性斑点病

[Pepper | Bacterial Spot] Q: What causes this?
A: Pepper bacterial spot is caused by Xanthomonas bacteria that can be carried on seed or transplants. Warm, humid conditions and splashing water favor infection and spread. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[辣椒 | 细菌性斑点病] 问：病因是什么？
答：辣椒细菌性斑点病由黄单胞菌引起，可通过种子或种苗传播。温暖高湿和溅水有利于侵染与扩散。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Pepper | Bacterial Spot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Leaves develop small angular water-soaked spots that turn brown, stems may form raised lesions, and fruit develops raised, rough, cracked brown spots. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[辣椒 | 细菌性斑点病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：叶片出现细小角形水渍状斑点并逐渐变褐，茎部可形成隆起病斑，果实出现隆起、粗糙、开裂的褐色斑点。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Pepper | Bacterial Spot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Start with pathogen-free seed or transplants, avoid overhead irrigation, rotate out of pepper, remove crop residue, and follow local guidance for copper or other registered bactericides where appropriate. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[辣椒 | 细菌性斑点病] 问：如何防治？
答：使用无病原种子或健康种苗，避免喷灌，与非寄主作物轮作并清除病残体；必要时根据当地建议使用登记的铜制剂或其他杀菌剂。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Pepper | Bacterial Spot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Pepper is a warm-season nightshade crop closely related to tomato. It responds best to steady moisture, healthy roots, good airflow, and early monitoring for insects that can also transmit viruses. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[辣椒 | 细菌性斑点病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：辣椒是与番茄近缘的喜温茄科作物。稳定水分、健康根系、良好通风以及及早监测可能传播病毒的害虫，对保持辣椒健康十分重要。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Pepper - Powdery Mildew / 辣椒 - 白粉病

[Pepper | Powdery Mildew] Q: What causes this?
A: Pepper powdery mildew is caused mainly by Leveillula taurica. Airborne spores spread the disease, which can become severe during warm conditions. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[辣椒 | 白粉病] 问：病因是什么？
答：辣椒白粉病主要由 Leveillula taurica 引起，病原孢子可随空气传播，在温暖条件下可能严重发生。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Pepper | Powdery Mildew] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: White powdery growth is often most visible on the lower leaf surface, while the upper surface develops yellow or brown patches. Infected leaves may curl upward and drop, exposing fruit to sunburn. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[辣椒 | 白粉病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：白色粉状霉层常主要出现在叶片背面，叶片正面出现黄色或褐色斑块；受害叶片可向上卷曲并脱落，使果实暴露后发生日灼。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Pepper | Powdery Mildew] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Scout regularly, improve airflow, remove heavily infected leaves where practical, and use locally appropriate resistant varieties or registered fungicides when disease pressure is high. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[辣椒 | 白粉病] 问：如何防治？
答：定期巡查，改善通风，适当清除严重病叶；病害压力高时可选用当地适宜的抗病品种或登记杀菌剂。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Pepper | Powdery Mildew] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Pepper is a warm-season nightshade crop closely related to tomato. It responds best to steady moisture, healthy roots, good airflow, and early monitoring for insects that can also transmit viruses. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[辣椒 | 白粉病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：辣椒是与番茄近缘的喜温茄科作物。稳定水分、健康根系、良好通风以及及早监测可能传播病毒的害虫，对保持辣椒健康十分重要。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Pepper - Whiteflies / 辣椒 - 白粉虱

[Pepper | Whiteflies] Q: What causes this?
A: Whiteflies reproduce on the undersides of pepper leaves and feed on phloem sap. Warm conditions and protected production can allow populations to build rapidly. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[辣椒 | 白粉虱] 问：病因是什么？
答：白粉虱在辣椒叶片背面繁殖并刺吸韧皮部汁液，温暖环境和设施栽培条件下种群可能迅速增加。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Pepper | Whiteflies] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Adults fly up when foliage is disturbed; immature stages remain attached beneath leaves. Heavy infestations cause honeydew, sooty mold, yellowing, reduced vigor, and may increase virus risk. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[辣椒 | 白粉虱] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：扰动叶片时成虫会飞起，若虫则附着在叶背。严重时出现蜜露、煤污、叶片黄化和长势下降，并可能增加病毒传播风险。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Pepper | Whiteflies] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use sticky traps and leaf inspections, remove heavily infested material, manage weeds, conserve natural enemies, and choose selective controls where treatment is needed. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[辣椒 | 白粉虱] 问：如何防治？
答：使用粘虫板和叶背检查进行监测，清除重度受害组织和杂草寄主，保护天敌，必要时优先选择对天敌影响较小的防治措施。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Pepper | Whiteflies] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Pepper is a warm-season nightshade crop closely related to tomato. It responds best to steady moisture, healthy roots, good airflow, and early monitoring for insects that can also transmit viruses. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[辣椒 | 白粉虱] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：辣椒是与番茄近缘的喜温茄科作物。稳定水分、健康根系、良好通风以及及早监测可能传播病毒的害虫，对保持辣椒健康十分重要。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Pepper - Blossom end rot / 辣椒 - 脐腐病

[Pepper | Blossom end rot] Q: What causes this?
A: Pepper blossom end rot is a physiological calcium-transport disorder usually associated with uneven moisture, root stress, high salinity, or rapid fruit growth rather than an infectious pathogen. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[辣椒 | 脐腐病] 问：病因是什么？
答：辣椒脐腐病是一种生理性钙运输障碍，通常与水分不稳定、根系受压、盐分过高或果实快速生长有关，并非由传染性病原引起。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Pepper | Blossom end rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: A pale or water-soaked area develops at the blossom end of fruit and becomes dark, sunken, dry, and leathery as the fruit enlarges. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[辣椒 | 脐腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：果实花端出现浅色或水渍状斑块，随着果实膨大逐渐变暗、凹陷、干燥并呈革质状。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Pepper | Blossom end rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Maintain steady soil moisture, avoid root injury and excessive fertilizer salts, mulch where suitable, and test soil before adding calcium. Damaged fruit will not recover. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[辣椒 | 脐腐病] 问：如何防治？
答：保持土壤水分稳定，避免伤根和肥盐过高，可适当覆盖地表；补钙前先检测土壤。已经受害的果实不会恢复。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Pepper | Blossom end rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Pepper is a warm-season nightshade crop closely related to tomato. It responds best to steady moisture, healthy roots, good airflow, and early monitoring for insects that can also transmit viruses. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[辣椒 | 脐腐病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：辣椒是与番茄近缘的喜温茄科作物。稳定水分、健康根系、良好通风以及及早监测可能传播病毒的害虫，对保持辣椒健康十分重要。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Pepper - Phytophthora Root and Crown Rot / 辣椒 - 疫霉根腐和冠腐病

[Pepper | Phytophthora Root and Crown Rot] Q: What causes this?
A: Phytophthora root and crown rot is caused by soilborne oomycetes favored by saturated, poorly drained soils and contaminated irrigation water. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[辣椒 | 疫霉根腐和冠腐病] 问：病因是什么？
答：辣椒疫霉根腐和冠腐病由土传疫霉类卵菌引起，积水、排水不良土壤和受污染灌溉水有利于病害发生。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Pepper | Phytophthora Root and Crown Rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Plants may wilt suddenly, lower stems or crowns become dark and water-soaked, roots rot, and severely affected plants collapse despite wet soil. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[辣椒 | 疫霉根腐和冠腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：植株可突然萎蔫，茎基部或根冠变暗并呈水渍状，根系腐烂，严重时即使土壤湿润植株也会倒伏死亡。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Pepper | Phytophthora Root and Crown Rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Improve drainage, avoid overirrigation and standing water, use healthy transplants and resistant material where available, rotate where practical, and follow local Phytophthora management guidance. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[辣椒 | 疫霉根腐和冠腐病] 问：如何防治？
答：改善排水，避免过度灌溉和积水，使用健康种苗和可利用的抗性材料，合理轮作，并遵循当地疫霉病防治建议。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Pepper | Phytophthora Root and Crown Rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Pepper is a warm-season nightshade crop closely related to tomato. It responds best to steady moisture, healthy roots, good airflow, and early monitoring for insects that can also transmit viruses. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[辣椒 | 疫霉根腐和冠腐病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：辣椒是与番茄近缘的喜温茄科作物。稳定水分、健康根系、良好通风以及及早监测可能传播病毒的害虫，对保持辣椒健康十分重要。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

# ===================================================================
# CUCUMBER / 黄瓜
# ===================================================================

## Cucumber - Viral Disease / 黄瓜 - 病毒病

[Cucumber | Viral Disease] Q: What causes this?
A: Cucumber mosaic virus (CMV) has an unusually wide host range and is transmitted mainly by aphids, which pick up the virus while feeding on infected plants or weeds and carry it to healthy cucumber plants. It also spreads through infected seed and mechanical contact during handling. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[黄瓜 | 病毒病] 问：病因是什么？
答：黄瓜花叶病毒（CMV）寄主范围异常广泛，主要通过蚜虫传播——蚜虫在感病植株或杂草上取食时获得病毒，再传染给健康的黄瓜植株。该病毒也可通过带毒种子及田间操作接触传播。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Cucumber | Viral Disease] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Symptoms vary by strain but commonly include a light-green to dark-green mottled pattern on leaves, vein banding, interveinal yellowing, and leaf distortion. In severe cases, dark green blisters can form, and symptoms can superficially resemble herbicide injury, which sometimes leads to misdiagnosis. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[黄瓜 | 病毒病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：症状因毒株不同而有差异，但常见表现为叶片呈浅绿至深绿相间的花叶斑驳、叶脉带状变色、脉间黄化及叶片畸形。严重时叶片会出现深绿色水泡状凸起，症状有时与除草剂药害相似，容易造成误诊。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Cucumber | Viral Disease] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Control the aphid populations that spread the virus between plants, remove and destroy infected plants promptly to reduce the source of new infections, and control weeds around the field since many weed species serve as a reservoir for the virus between cropping seasons. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[黄瓜 | 病毒病] 问：如何防治？
答：控制传播病毒的蚜虫种群；及时拔除并销毁感病植株，以减少新的传染源；同时清除田边杂草，因为许多杂草是该病毒在作物茬口之间的越冬寄主和储存库。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Cucumber | Viral Disease] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cucumber belongs to the cucurbit family and, because CMV can also infect beans, peppers, and many ornamental and weed species, managing this virus effectively usually requires attention to what is growing in and around the field, not just the cucumber crop itself. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[黄瓜 | 病毒病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：黄瓜属于葫芦科作物。由于黄瓜花叶病毒同样能感染豆类、辣椒以及多种观赏植物和杂草，因此要有效控制该病毒，通常需要关注田块内外的整体种植环境，而不仅仅是黄瓜本身。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Cucumber - Mites / 黄瓜 - 螨

[Cucumber | Mites] Q: What causes this?
A: Spider mites on cucumber build up during hot, dry conditions, particularly in high tunnels or greenhouses where humidity is low. Populations can escalate quickly once conditions favor them, especially if natural predators have been reduced by earlier pesticide applications. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[黄瓜 | 螨] 问：病因是什么？
答：黄瓜上红蜘蛛（叶螨）多在炎热干燥的条件下大量发生，尤其是在湿度较低的大棚或温室中更为常见。一旦条件适宜，种群会迅速扩增，若此前的农药施用减少了天敌数量，情况会更加严重。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Cucumber | Mites] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Early infestation shows as light stippling on the upper leaf surface, with the mites themselves visible on the underside if you look closely. As populations grow, webbing can cover entire leaves and branches, and heavily infested plants may show overall bronzing or curling of foliage. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[黄瓜 | 螨] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：早期侵染表现为叶片正面出现轻微的白色斑点，仔细查看叶片背面即可发现螨虫本身。随着种群增长，蛛网可能覆盖整片叶子和枝条，严重受害的植株叶片会出现整体古铜色或卷曲现象。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Cucumber | Mites] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Treat infestations as soon as they are found at any level, since dry conditions inside tunnels or greenhouses let mite populations multiply rapidly if left unchecked. Materials that spare beneficial predatory mites and insects are preferred when natural enemies are still present in the planting. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[黄瓜 | 螨] 问：如何防治？
答：一旦发现螨虫，无论密度高低都应及时处理，因为大棚或温室内的干燥环境会让螨虫种群在不加控制的情况下迅速倍增。若田间仍有天敌存在，应优先选用对捕食性螨类和益虫较温和的防治药剂。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Cucumber | Mites] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cucumber grown in high tunnels or greenhouses faces a higher spider mite risk than field-grown cucumber specifically because of the drier, more sheltered growing environment, so growers using protected culture should scout for mites more frequently. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[黄瓜 | 螨] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：与露地种植的黄瓜相比，大棚或温室栽培的黄瓜面临更高的红蜘蛛风险，这正是由于设施内更干燥、更封闭的生长环境所致，因此设施栽培的种植者应更频繁地巡查螨情。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Cucumber - Downy Mildew / 黄瓜 - 霜霉病

[Cucumber | Downy Mildew] Q: What causes this?
A: Cucurbit downy mildew is caused by a water-mold pathogen that spreads through the air over long distances and establishes quickly once it reaches a field, particularly on cucumber and cantaloupe, which are more susceptible than other cucurbits to the strain that affects this crop group. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[黄瓜 | 霜霉病] 问：病因是什么？
答：黄瓜霜霉病由一种卵菌类病原引起，该病原可通过空气远距离传播，一旦到达田间便能迅速定殖，其中黄瓜和甜瓜对该病原所对应的致病型尤为易感，比其他葫芦科作物更容易发病。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Cucumber | Downy Mildew] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for angular, yellow spots on the upper leaf surface that are bounded by leaf veins, giving them a blocky rather than round shape. On the underside of the leaf, a grayish or purplish fuzzy growth may be visible during humid conditions, especially in the early morning. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[黄瓜 | 霜霉病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：注意叶片正面出现受叶脉限制、呈方块状而非圆形的黄色角斑。在潮湿条件下，尤其是清晨，叶片背面可能可见灰色或紫灰色的霉状物。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Cucumber | Downy Mildew] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Choose downy-mildew-resistant cucumber varieties where available, since resistant varieties can extend the harvest window by several weeks compared to susceptible ones. Apply protective fungicides preventively once downy mildew has been detected or forecast in the surrounding region, and scout regularly during humid periods. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[黄瓜 | 霜霉病] 问：如何防治？
答：尽量选用抗霜霉病的黄瓜品种，与感病品种相比，抗性品种可延长数周的采收期。一旦周边地区检测到或预测有霜霉病发生，应及时预防性喷施保护性杀菌剂，并在潮湿季节加强田间巡查。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Cucumber | Downy Mildew] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cucumber is among the cucurbit crops most susceptible to downy mildew, and because the pathogen spreads on wind currents across large distances, regional disease alerts and forecasting systems are an important tool for timing protective fungicide sprays correctly. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[黄瓜 | 霜霉病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
