答：黄瓜是葫芦科作物中对霜霉病最为易感的品种之一，由于该病原可随气流远距离传播，区域性的病害预警和预测系统是把握保护性杀菌剂正确施用时机的重要工具。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Cucumber - Aphids / 黄瓜 - 蚜虫

[Cucumber | Aphids] Q: What causes this?
A: Melon aphid and green peach aphid are the main species affecting cucumber, colonizing the undersides of leaves and new growth. Populations expand quickly in mild weather and can be reintroduced repeatedly from nearby weeds or other host crops throughout the season. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[黄瓜 | 蚜虫] 问：病因是什么？
答：为害黄瓜的主要蚜虫种类是瓜蚜和桃蚜，它们聚集在叶片背面和新梢上。在温和天气下种群扩增迅速，并可能在整个生长季节中反复从附近的杂草或其他寄主作物迁入。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Cucumber | Aphids] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for colonies of small soft-bodied insects under leaves, sticky honeydew residue on foliage, sooty mold growing on the honeydew, curled or distorted new leaves, and stunted growth on heavily infested plants. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[黄瓜 | 蚜虫] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：留意叶片背面成群的小型软体昆虫、叶面上粘性的蜜露残留物、蜜露上滋生的煤污病、卷曲或畸形的新叶，以及重度受害植株的生长迟缓。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Cucumber | Aphids] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Monitor the underside of leaves weekly rather than relying only on sticky-trap counts, and encourage or introduce natural predators such as lady beetles and aphid parasites where infestation levels are still moderate. Controlling nearby weed hosts reduces the number of aphids migrating into the planting. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[黄瓜 | 蚜虫] 问：如何防治？
答：每周检查叶片背面进行监测，不要仅依靠粘虫板计数；在虫害程度尚属中等时，可引入或保护瓢虫、蚜虫寄生蜂等天敌。清除周边杂草寄主可减少迁入田间的蚜虫数量。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Cucumber | Aphids] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Because aphids are also the primary vector for cucumber mosaic virus, aphid management on cucumber serves a dual purpose: limiting direct feeding damage and reducing the risk of viral disease spreading through the planting. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[黄瓜 | 蚜虫] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：由于蚜虫同时也是黄瓜花叶病毒的主要传播媒介，因此黄瓜上的蚜虫防治具有双重意义：既能减少直接取食造成的损害，也能降低病毒病在田间传播的风险。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Cucumber - Anthracnose / 黄瓜 - 炭疽病

[Cucumber | Anthracnose] Q: What causes this?
A: Cucumber anthracnose is caused by Colletotrichum fungal species and, as with pepper, infection can occur on fruit while it is still developing in the field even though visible symptoms may not appear until later, including after harvest. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[黄瓜 | 炭疽病] 问：病因是什么？
答：黄瓜炭疽病由炭疽菌（Colletotrichum）引起，与辣椒炭疽病类似，感染可能在果实尚在田间发育时就已发生，但可见症状可能要到之后（包括采收后）才显现出来。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Cucumber | Anthracnose] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for water-soaked lesions that expand into sunken, dark spots on leaves and fruit, sometimes developing concentric rings of spore masses within the lesion as the infection matures. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[黄瓜 | 炭疽病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：留意叶片和果实上出现水浸状病斑，逐渐扩大为凹陷的暗色斑点，随着感染成熟，病斑内有时会形成同心圆状分布的孢子堆。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Cucumber | Anthracnose] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use clean seed and rotate cucumber away from other cucurbits to break the disease cycle in the soil, and apply fungicide sprays preventively during warm, humid periods when the disease is most likely to establish. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[黄瓜 | 炭疽病] 问：如何防治？
答：使用洁净种子，并与其他葫芦科作物轮作以打断土壤中的病害循环；在病害最容易发生的温暖潮湿季节，预防性喷施杀菌剂。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Cucumber | Anthracnose] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cucumber shares several major fungal diseases, including anthracnose and downy mildew, with other cucurbit crops such as melon and squash, so crop rotation planning needs to account for the whole cucurbit family, not cucumber alone. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[黄瓜 | 炭疽病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：黄瓜与甜瓜、南瓜等其他葫芦科作物共有多种主要真菌病害，包括炭疽病和霜霉病，因此在制定轮作计划时，需要考虑整个葫芦科作物群体，而不仅仅是黄瓜本身。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Cucumber - Powdery Mildew / 黄瓜 - 白粉病

[Cucumber | Powdery Mildew] Q: What causes this?
A: Cucumber powdery mildew is caused by powdery mildew fungi whose airborne spores readily infect cucurbit foliage, especially in dense canopies and moderate to warm conditions. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[黄瓜 | 白粉病] 问：病因是什么？
答：黄瓜白粉病由白粉菌类真菌引起，孢子可随空气传播，在冠层茂密和适中至温暖条件下容易侵染葫芦科叶片。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Cucumber | Powdery Mildew] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: White powdery patches appear on leaves and stems, expand, and can cause yellowing, browning, premature leaf death, and reduced fruit quality. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[黄瓜 | 白粉病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：叶片和茎上出现白色粉状斑块并逐渐扩大，严重时叶片黄化、褐变和提前枯死，并影响果实品质。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Cucumber | Powdery Mildew] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use resistant varieties where available, improve airflow, remove severely affected leaves, and apply locally registered fungicides preventively or early when disease pressure warrants. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[黄瓜 | 白粉病] 问：如何防治？
答：尽量使用抗病品种，改善通风，清除严重病叶；病害压力较高时按当地建议预防或早期使用登记杀菌剂。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Cucumber | Powdery Mildew] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cucumber is a fast-growing cucurbit that benefits from good airflow, careful irrigation, healthy pollination, and frequent scouting because foliar diseases and sucking insects can spread quickly in dense canopies. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[黄瓜 | 白粉病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：黄瓜是生长迅速的葫芦科作物，需要良好通风、合理灌溉和正常授粉。由于叶部病害和刺吸式害虫可在茂密冠层中迅速扩散，应经常巡查。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Cucumber - Angular Leaf Spot / 黄瓜 - 角斑病

[Cucumber | Angular Leaf Spot] Q: What causes this?
A: Angular leaf spot is caused by Pseudomonas syringae pv. lachrymans. The bacterium can survive in infected debris or seed and spreads rapidly through rain, overhead irrigation, workers, and equipment in humid conditions. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[黄瓜 | 角斑病] 问：病因是什么？
答：黄瓜角斑病由 Pseudomonas syringae pv. lachrymans 引起，病原可在病残体或种子中存活，在高湿条件下通过雨水、喷灌、人员和设备迅速传播。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Cucumber | Angular Leaf Spot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Water-soaked spots become gray or tan and are bounded by veins, giving them an angular shape. Dry tissue may fall out, leaving a tattered appearance; fruit lesions are often superficial. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[黄瓜 | 角斑病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：水渍状病斑逐渐变成灰色或黄褐色，并受叶脉限制呈角形；病组织干枯后可脱落形成破孔，果实病斑通常较浅表。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Cucumber | Angular Leaf Spot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use resistant varieties and clean seed, limit overhead irrigation, avoid working plants when wet, remove infected debris, and sanitize equipment between affected areas. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[黄瓜 | 角斑病] 问：如何防治？
答：使用抗病品种和健康种子，减少喷灌，植株潮湿时避免田间操作，清除病残体，并在受害区域之间清洁消毒设备。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Cucumber | Angular Leaf Spot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cucumber is a fast-growing cucurbit that benefits from good airflow, careful irrigation, healthy pollination, and frequent scouting because foliar diseases and sucking insects can spread quickly in dense canopies. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[黄瓜 | 角斑病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：黄瓜是生长迅速的葫芦科作物，需要良好通风、合理灌溉和正常授粉。由于叶部病害和刺吸式害虫可在茂密冠层中迅速扩散，应经常巡查。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Cucumber - Whiteflies / 黄瓜 - 白粉虱

[Cucumber | Whiteflies] Q: What causes this?
A: Whiteflies colonize cucumber leaf undersides and feed on plant sap. Warm greenhouse or field conditions can support rapid population growth. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[黄瓜 | 白粉虱] 问：病因是什么？
答：白粉虱在黄瓜叶片背面聚集并刺吸汁液，温暖的温室或田间环境可使种群迅速增长。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Cucumber | Whiteflies] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for white adults flying from disturbed leaves, immature stages beneath leaves, honeydew, sooty mold, yellowing, and reduced growth. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[黄瓜 | 白粉虱] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：扰动叶片时可见白色成虫飞起，叶背有若虫，同时可能出现蜜露、煤污、叶片黄化和生长受抑。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Cucumber | Whiteflies] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Monitor with sticky traps and leaf inspections, remove heavily infested leaves, control weeds, conserve natural enemies, and use selective treatments if populations become damaging. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[黄瓜 | 白粉虱] 问：如何防治？
答：使用粘虫板和叶背检查监测，清除重度受害叶片和杂草寄主，保护天敌，虫口密度造成明显危害时再采取选择性防治。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Cucumber | Whiteflies] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cucumber is a fast-growing cucurbit that benefits from good airflow, careful irrigation, healthy pollination, and frequent scouting because foliar diseases and sucking insects can spread quickly in dense canopies. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[黄瓜 | 白粉虱] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：黄瓜是生长迅速的葫芦科作物，需要良好通风、合理灌溉和正常授粉。由于叶部病害和刺吸式害虫可在茂密冠层中迅速扩散，应经常巡查。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Cucumber - Thrips / 黄瓜 - 蓟马

[Cucumber | Thrips] Q: What causes this?
A: Thrips feed by rasping cucumber leaves, flowers, and young fruit. Populations often increase during warm, dry periods and can move in from surrounding vegetation. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[黄瓜 | 蓟马] 问：病因是什么？
答：蓟马通过锉吸方式危害黄瓜叶片、花和幼果，在温暖干燥时期种群常增加，并可从周边植被迁入。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Cucumber | Thrips] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Feeding causes silvery or pale scarring, tiny black fecal specks, distorted young leaves or flowers, and surface scarring on developing fruit. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[黄瓜 | 蓟马] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：取食会造成银白色或浅色擦伤斑、细小黑色粪点、新叶或花器畸形，以及幼果表面的疤痕。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Cucumber | Thrips] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use sticky traps and flower/leaf inspections, reduce nearby weed hosts, conserve predatory insects and mites, and use selective controls only when monitoring shows damaging populations. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[黄瓜 | 蓟马] 问：如何防治？
答：使用粘虫板并检查花和叶片，减少周边杂草寄主，保护捕食性昆虫和螨类，仅在监测显示虫口密度达到危害水平时采取选择性防治。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Cucumber | Thrips] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cucumber is a fast-growing cucurbit that benefits from good airflow, careful irrigation, healthy pollination, and frequent scouting because foliar diseases and sucking insects can spread quickly in dense canopies. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[黄瓜 | 蓟马] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：黄瓜是生长迅速的葫芦科作物，需要良好通风、合理灌溉和正常授粉。由于叶部病害和刺吸式害虫可在茂密冠层中迅速扩散，应经常巡查。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Cucumber - Root Rot / 黄瓜 - 根腐病

[Cucumber | Root Rot] Q: What causes this?
A: Cucumber root rot can be caused by several soilborne pathogens, including oomycetes and fungi, and is favored by saturated soil, poor drainage, root injury, and continuous cropping. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[黄瓜 | 根腐病] 问：病因是什么？
答：黄瓜根腐病可由多种土传真菌或卵菌引起，土壤长期过湿、排水不良、根系受伤和连作都会增加发病风险。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Cucumber | Root Rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Plants become weak, yellow, and wilted even when water is available; roots turn brown, soft, or sparse, and the crown may discolor as disease progresses. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[黄瓜 | 根腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：植株长势衰弱、黄化并萎蔫，即使有水也难恢复；根系变褐、变软或数量减少，病情发展后根冠也可变色。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Cucumber | Root Rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Improve drainage, avoid overwatering, use clean growing media and healthy transplants, rotate when possible, remove severely affected plants, and disinfect reused equipment or containers. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[黄瓜 | 根腐病] 问：如何防治？
答：改善排水，避免过度浇水，使用清洁基质和健康种苗，条件允许时轮作，清除严重病株，并对重复使用的设备和容器进行清洁消毒。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Cucumber | Root Rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cucumber is a fast-growing cucurbit that benefits from good airflow, careful irrigation, healthy pollination, and frequent scouting because foliar diseases and sucking insects can spread quickly in dense canopies. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[黄瓜 | 根腐病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：黄瓜是生长迅速的葫芦科作物，需要良好通风、合理灌溉和正常授粉。由于叶部病害和刺吸式害虫可在茂密冠层中迅速扩散，应经常巡查。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

# ===================================================================
# EGGPLANT / 茄子
# ===================================================================

## Eggplant - Mites / 茄子 - 螨

[Eggplant | Mites] Q: What causes this?
A: Spider mites infest eggplant under the same hot, dry conditions that favor mites on other nightshade crops like tomato and pepper. Reduced numbers of natural predator insects, often following broad-spectrum insecticide use, also allow mite populations to build up unchecked. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[茄子 | 螨] 问：病因是什么？
答：茄子上红蜘蛛的侵染与番茄、辣椒等其他茄科作物一样，是在炎热干燥的条件下发生的。此前使用广谱杀虫剂导致天敌昆虫数量减少，也会使螨类种群失去制约而大量繁殖。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Eggplant | Mites] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for fine stippling on the upper leaf surface and check the underside of leaves for the mites themselves, since they are difficult to see without close inspection. Heavy infestations produce visible webbing and can cause leaves to bronze, curl, or drop prematurely. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[茄子 | 螨] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：留意叶片正面出现的细小白色斑点，并检查叶片背面是否有螨虫本身，因为不仔细观察很难发现它们。严重侵染时会产生明显的蛛网，并可能导致叶片古铜色、卷曲或提前脱落。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Eggplant | Mites] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Treat as soon as mites are detected at any level, since hot conditions allow rapid population growth once an infestation starts. Choose miticides that spare beneficial predatory mites and insects when natural enemies are still present in the field. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[茄子 | 螨] 问：如何防治？
答：一旦发现螨虫迹象，无论密度多少都应立即处理，因为在炎热条件下侵染一旦开始，种群会迅速增长。若田间仍有天敌存在，应选用对捕食性螨类和益虫较为温和的杀螨剂。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Eggplant | Mites] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Eggplant is a nightshade crop, closely related to tomato and pepper, and shares many of the same insect and mite pressures as those crops, which is why growers who rotate among nightshades still need to scout each crop separately for mites. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[茄子 | 螨] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：茄子是茄科作物，与番茄、辣椒近缘，面临与这些作物相似的虫害和螨害压力，因此即使在茄科作物之间轮作，种植者仍需对每种作物分别进行螨情巡查。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Eggplant - Aphids / 茄子 - 蚜虫

[Eggplant | Aphids] Q: What causes this?
A: Aphids colonize eggplant shoots and leaf undersides and reproduce rapidly under favorable weather. Their feeding removes sap and produces honeydew. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[茄子 | 蚜虫] 问：病因是什么？
答：蚜虫聚集在茄子嫩梢和叶片背面，在适宜天气下繁殖迅速，通过刺吸汁液造成危害并分泌蜜露。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Eggplant | Aphids] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for clusters of soft-bodied insects, curled or distorted young leaves, sticky honeydew, sooty mold, and reduced vigor. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[茄子 | 蚜虫] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：可见成群软体小虫、新叶卷曲或畸形、粘性蜜露、煤污以及植株长势下降。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Eggplant | Aphids] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Inspect new growth, conserve lady beetles, lacewings, and parasitoids, reduce weed hosts, use reflective mulch where suitable, and avoid unnecessary broad-spectrum insecticides. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[茄子 | 蚜虫] 问：如何防治？
答：检查嫩梢，保护瓢虫、草蛉和寄生蜂等天敌，减少杂草寄主，条件适宜时使用反光地膜，并避免不必要的广谱杀虫剂。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Eggplant | Aphids] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Eggplant is a warm-season nightshade crop related to tomato and pepper. Healthy transplants, crop rotation, warm growing conditions, and regular inspection of leaf undersides help reduce common wilt diseases and insect problems. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[茄子 | 蚜虫] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：茄子是与番茄和辣椒近缘的喜温茄科作物。使用健康种苗、合理轮作、保持适宜温度，并经常检查叶片背面，有助于减少枯萎病和常见虫害。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Eggplant - Whiteflies / 茄子 - 白粉虱

[Eggplant | Whiteflies] Q: What causes this?
A: Whiteflies feed and reproduce on eggplant leaf undersides, and warm conditions can produce large populations quickly. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[茄子 | 白粉虱] 问：病因是什么？
答：白粉虱在茄子叶片背面取食和繁殖，温暖条件下种群可迅速增加。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Eggplant | Whiteflies] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Adults fly when leaves are disturbed, immature stages remain beneath leaves, and heavy infestations lead to honeydew, sooty mold, yellowing, and reduced plant vigor. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[茄子 | 白粉虱] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：扰动叶片时成虫飞起，若虫附着在叶背；严重时出现蜜露、煤污、叶片黄化和植株衰弱。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Eggplant | Whiteflies] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Monitor with sticky traps and leaf inspections, remove heavily infested material, control weed hosts, conserve natural enemies, and use selective controls if needed. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[茄子 | 白粉虱] 问：如何防治？
答：使用粘虫板和叶背检查监测，清除重度受害组织和杂草寄主，保护天敌，必要时采用选择性防治措施。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Eggplant | Whiteflies] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Eggplant is a warm-season nightshade crop related to tomato and pepper. Healthy transplants, crop rotation, warm growing conditions, and regular inspection of leaf undersides help reduce common wilt diseases and insect problems. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[茄子 | 白粉虱] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：茄子是与番茄和辣椒近缘的喜温茄科作物。使用健康种苗、合理轮作、保持适宜温度，并经常检查叶片背面，有助于减少枯萎病和常见虫害。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Eggplant - Flea Beetles / 茄子 - 跳甲

[Eggplant | Flea Beetles] Q: What causes this?
A: Flea beetles are small jumping beetles that chew eggplant foliage, with seedlings and young plants especially vulnerable. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[茄子 | 跳甲] 问：病因是什么？
答：跳甲是体型很小、善于跳跃的甲虫，会啃食茄子叶片，幼苗和幼株尤其容易受害。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Eggplant | Flea Beetles] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Feeding produces many tiny round or irregular 'shot holes' in leaves. Heavy feeding can weaken young plants and reduce growth. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[茄子 | 跳甲] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：取食后叶片出现大量细小圆形或不规则的“弹孔状”小孔，严重时会削弱幼株并影响生长。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Eggplant | Flea Beetles] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Protect young plants with physical barriers where practical, control nearby weed hosts, monitor seedlings closely, and use registered controls only when feeding threatens establishment. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[茄子 | 跳甲] 问：如何防治？
答：幼苗期可在适当情况下使用物理隔离措施，清除周边杂草寄主并加强巡查；仅在取食明显影响成活和生长时采用登记防治措施。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Eggplant | Flea Beetles] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Eggplant is a warm-season nightshade crop related to tomato and pepper. Healthy transplants, crop rotation, warm growing conditions, and regular inspection of leaf undersides help reduce common wilt diseases and insect problems. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[茄子 | 跳甲] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：茄子是与番茄和辣椒近缘的喜温茄科作物。使用健康种苗、合理轮作、保持适宜温度，并经常检查叶片背面，有助于减少枯萎病和常见虫害。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Eggplant - Verticillium Wilt / 茄子 - 黄萎病

[Eggplant | Verticillium Wilt] Q: What causes this?
A: Verticillium wilt is caused by soilborne Verticillium fungi that enter roots and colonize vascular tissue. Resting structures can persist in soil for many years. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[茄子 | 黄萎病] 问：病因是什么？
答：茄子黄萎病由土传轮枝菌引起，病原从根部侵入并定殖维管组织，其休眠结构可在土壤中存活多年。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Eggplant | Verticillium Wilt] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Lower leaves yellow and wilt first, often asymmetrically or on one side of the plant. Symptoms progress upward, and a cut stem may show tan to brown vascular discoloration. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[茄子 | 黄萎病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：下部叶片先黄化萎蔫，常表现为不对称或先发生在植株一侧，随后向上发展；切开茎部可见黄褐至褐色维管组织变色。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Eggplant | Verticillium Wilt] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use clean transplants and resistant material where available, avoid moving infested soil, rotate for multiple years with nonhost crops where practical, and remove severely affected plants. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[茄子 | 黄萎病] 问：如何防治？
答：使用健康种苗和可利用的抗性材料，避免携带污染土壤；条件允许时与非寄主作物进行多年轮作，并清除严重病株。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Eggplant | Verticillium Wilt] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Eggplant is a warm-season nightshade crop related to tomato and pepper. Healthy transplants, crop rotation, warm growing conditions, and regular inspection of leaf undersides help reduce common wilt diseases and insect problems. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[茄子 | 黄萎病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：茄子是与番茄和辣椒近缘的喜温茄科作物。使用健康种苗、合理轮作、保持适宜温度，并经常检查叶片背面，有助于减少枯萎病和常见虫害。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Eggplant - Mosaic Virus / 茄子 - 花叶病毒病

[Eggplant | Mosaic Virus] Q: What causes this?
A: Eggplant mosaic symptoms can be caused by several viruses, including cucumber mosaic virus and related viruses, spread by insect vectors or contaminated plant material depending on the virus. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[茄子 | 花叶病毒病] 问：病因是什么？
答：茄子花叶症状可由多种病毒引起，包括黄瓜花叶病毒等，不同病毒可通过昆虫媒介或受污染的植物材料传播。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Eggplant | Mosaic Virus] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Leaves develop light- and dark-green mottling, distortion, curling, or unusually narrow 'shoestring' growth, and plants may become stunted. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[茄子 | 花叶病毒病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：叶片出现深浅绿色相间的花叶斑驳、畸形、卷曲或异常狭长的“线叶”症状，植株也可能矮化。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Eggplant | Mosaic Virus] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use healthy transplants, remove symptomatic plants, control weeds that can host viruses and vectors, manage relevant insect vectors, and sanitize tools where mechanical spread is possible. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[茄子 | 花叶病毒病] 问：如何防治？
答：使用健康种苗，及时清除有症状植株，控制可作为病毒和媒介寄主的杂草，管理相关传毒害虫，并在可能机械传播时清洁消毒工具。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Eggplant | Mosaic Virus] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Eggplant is a warm-season nightshade crop related to tomato and pepper. Healthy transplants, crop rotation, warm growing conditions, and regular inspection of leaf undersides help reduce common wilt diseases and insect problems. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[茄子 | 花叶病毒病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：茄子是与番茄和辣椒近缘的喜温茄科作物。使用健康种苗、合理轮作、保持适宜温度，并经常检查叶片背面，有助于减少枯萎病和常见虫害。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

# CORN / 玉米
# ===================================================================

## Corn - Chemical/Pesticide Damage / 玉米 - 药害

[Corn | Chemical Damage] Q: What causes this?
A: Chemical damage (phytotoxicity) in corn is most often caused by herbicide drift from a nearby application, herbicide carryover from a previous season or crop, tank contamination from residue left over in spray equipment, or applying a chemical while the crop is under environmental stress. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[玉米 | 药害] 问：病因是什么？
答：玉米药害（植物毒害）最常见的原因包括附近喷药作业造成的飘移、上一季或上茬作物残留的除草剂、喷药设备清洗不彻底造成的药箱污染，以及在作物处于环境胁迫状态下施药。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Corn | Chemical Damage] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Symptoms depend on the herbicide's mode of action: some cause malformed brace roots, brittle stems, and stunting; others cause stunted growth with yellowing in the youngest leaves and reddened veins; and some cause leaves that fail to unroll properly, an injury pattern sometimes called "buggy-whipping." Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[玉米 | 药害] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：症状因除草剂作用机制不同而异：有些会导致支持根畸形、茎秆变脆及植株矮化；有些会导致植株矮化并伴有最幼嫩叶片黄化、叶脉发红；还有些会导致叶片无法正常展开，形成俗称"鞭状卷叶"的受害症状。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Corn | Chemical Damage] Q: How can I manage it?
A: There is no way to reverse chemical damage once it has occurred, so management is about diagnosis and prevention: identify the likely herbicide by matching symptoms to its mode of action, review recent spray records and nearby field activity, and clean spray equipment thoroughly between different chemical applications to prevent tank contamination. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[玉米 | 药害] 问：如何防治？
答：药害一旦发生便无法逆转，因此管理重点在于诊断和预防：根据症状对应除草剂的作用机制来判断可能的致害药剂，查阅近期施药记录及周边地块的作业情况，并在更换不同药剂时彻底清洗喷药设备以防止药箱污染。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Corn | Chemical Damage] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Corn's tolerance to different herbicides varies significantly depending on the specific product and its mode of action, so what is a safe, labeled application for corn can still cause visible injury under certain weather or soil conditions, or if used at the wrong growth stage. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[玉米 | 药害] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：玉米对不同除草剂的耐受性因产品及其作用机制的不同而有明显差异，因此即使是对玉米标注为安全的合规用药，在特定天气或土壤条件下，或在错误的生育阶段使用，仍可能造成明显药害。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Corn - Northern Corn Leaf Blight / 玉米 - 大斑病

[Corn | Northern Corn Leaf Blight] Q: What causes this?
A: Northern corn leaf blight is caused by Exserohilum turcicum, which survives in infected corn residue. Moderate temperatures and prolonged leaf wetness favor disease development. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[玉米 | 大斑病] 问：病因是什么？
答：玉米大斑病由 Exserohilum turcicum 引起，病原可在带病玉米残体中越冬，适中温度和叶片长时间潮湿有利于发病。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Corn | Northern Corn Leaf Blight] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Long gray-green lesions develop into tan, cigar- or canoe-shaped lesions that can reach several inches in length, usually beginning on lower leaves and moving upward. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[玉米 | 大斑病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：叶片出现灰绿色长斑，随后变成黄褐色、雪茄形或舟形大斑，可长达数英寸，通常从下部叶片开始向上发展。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Corn | Northern Corn Leaf Blight] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Plant resistant hybrids, rotate crops, manage infected residue where appropriate, scout before and after silking, and consider fungicides only when hybrid susceptibility, disease level, and economics justify them. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[玉米 | 大斑病] 问：如何防治？
答：种植抗病杂交种，合理轮作并管理病残体，在抽丝前后加强巡查；只有在品种感病、病害程度和经济效益均支持时才考虑使用杀菌剂。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Corn | Northern Corn Leaf Blight] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Corn health depends on hybrid resistance, residue and rotation management, balanced fertility, and regular scouting from vegetative growth through grain fill because foliar diseases and insects can reduce photosynthetic leaf area. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[玉米 | 大斑病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：玉米健康与品种抗性、残体和轮作管理、均衡施肥以及从营养生长期到灌浆期的持续巡查密切相关，因为叶部病害和害虫会减少有效光合叶面积。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Corn - Gray Leaf Spot / 玉米 - 灰斑病

[Corn | Gray Leaf Spot] Q: What causes this?
A: Gray leaf spot is caused by Cercospora zeae-maydis and is favored by humid, wet conditions, susceptible hybrids, and infected residue in continuous or reduced-tillage corn. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[玉米 | 灰斑病] 问：病因是什么？
答：玉米灰斑病由 Cercospora zeae-maydis 引起，高湿多雨、感病品种以及连作或少耕条件下残留的带病残体有利于发病。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Corn | Gray Leaf Spot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Lesions become rectangular, gray to tan, and run parallel between leaf veins. Severe disease reduces green leaf area during grain fill. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[玉米 | 灰斑病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：病斑逐渐呈矩形、灰色至黄褐色，并沿叶脉平行分布；严重时在灌浆期大量减少绿色叶面积。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Corn | Gray Leaf Spot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use resistant hybrids, rotate away from corn, manage residue where suitable, scout around tasseling and grain fill, and use fungicides only when risk and expected benefit justify treatment. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[玉米 | 灰斑病] 问：如何防治？
答：使用抗病品种，与非玉米作物轮作，并在适当情况下管理残体；抽雄和灌浆期加强巡查，仅在风险和预期收益合理时使用杀菌剂。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Corn | Gray Leaf Spot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Corn health depends on hybrid resistance, residue and rotation management, balanced fertility, and regular scouting from vegetative growth through grain fill because foliar diseases and insects can reduce photosynthetic leaf area. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[玉米 | 灰斑病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：玉米健康与品种抗性、残体和轮作管理、均衡施肥以及从营养生长期到灌浆期的持续巡查密切相关，因为叶部病害和害虫会减少有效光合叶面积。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Corn - Common Rust / 玉米 - 普通锈病

[Corn | Common Rust] Q: What causes this?
A: Common rust is caused by Puccinia sorghi. Airborne spores infect corn, and cool to moderate humid conditions favor development. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[玉米 | 普通锈病] 问：病因是什么？
答：玉米普通锈病由 Puccinia sorghi 引起，孢子可随空气传播，凉爽至适中且潮湿的环境有利于发病。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Corn | Common Rust] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Small chlorotic flecks develop into elongated, blister-like pustules containing cinnamon-brown to brick-red spores, often on both leaf surfaces. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[玉米 | 普通锈病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：叶片先出现浅色小斑点，随后形成细长、疱状的孢子堆，内含肉桂褐至砖红色孢子，常在叶片正反两面出现。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Corn | Common Rust] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Plant resistant hybrids when possible and scout susceptible corn. Fungicides are generally considered only when disease appears early and conditions, crop value, and susceptibility indicate meaningful risk. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[玉米 | 普通锈病] 问：如何防治？
答：尽量种植抗病杂交种，并加强感病玉米巡查。通常只有在病害出现较早且环境、作物价值和品种感病性表明风险较高时才考虑使用杀菌剂。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Corn | Common Rust] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Corn health depends on hybrid resistance, residue and rotation management, balanced fertility, and regular scouting from vegetative growth through grain fill because foliar diseases and insects can reduce photosynthetic leaf area. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[玉米 | 普通锈病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：玉米健康与品种抗性、残体和轮作管理、均衡施肥以及从营养生长期到灌浆期的持续巡查密切相关，因为叶部病害和害虫会减少有效光合叶面积。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Corn - Fall Armyworm / 玉米 - 草地贪夜蛾

[Corn | Fall Armyworm] Q: What causes this?
A: Fall armyworm damage is caused by Spodoptera frugiperda larvae feeding in whorls, leaves, tassels, and ears. Warm conditions allow repeated generations and rapid population growth. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[玉米 | 草地贪夜蛾] 问：病因是什么？
答：草地贪夜蛾由 Spodoptera frugiperda 幼虫危害，幼虫可在心叶、叶片、雄穗和果穗取食，温暖条件下可连续繁殖并迅速增加。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Corn | Fall Armyworm] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Young leaves show windowpane feeding and ragged holes, whorls may contain frass, and larger larvae can damage tassels or ears. Larvae often have an inverted Y-shaped marking on the head. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[玉米 | 草地贪夜蛾] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：幼叶可见窗格状取食痕和不规则孔洞，心叶内常有大量虫粪，较大幼虫还可危害雄穗和果穗；幼虫头部常有倒Y形纹。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Corn | Fall Armyworm] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Scout whorls and young plants, conserve natural enemies, destroy crop residues where locally appropriate, and use threshold-based registered controls when larval feeding is economically damaging. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[玉米 | 草地贪夜蛾] 问：如何防治？
答：检查心叶和幼株，保护天敌，在当地适宜时处理作物残体；当幼虫取食达到经济危害水平时再采用基于阈值的登记防治措施。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Corn | Fall Armyworm] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Corn health depends on hybrid resistance, residue and rotation management, balanced fertility, and regular scouting from vegetative growth through grain fill because foliar diseases and insects can reduce photosynthetic leaf area. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[玉米 | 草地贪夜蛾] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：玉米健康与品种抗性、残体和轮作管理、均衡施肥以及从营养生长期到灌浆期的持续巡查密切相关，因为叶部病害和害虫会减少有效光合叶面积。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Corn - Aphids / 玉米 - 蚜虫

[Corn | Aphids] Q: What causes this?
A: Corn aphids and other aphid species colonize leaves, tassels, and whorls and feed on plant sap. Populations often build rapidly when natural enemies are scarce. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[玉米 | 蚜虫] 问：病因是什么？
答：玉米蚜及其他蚜虫可聚集在叶片、雄穗和心叶处刺吸汁液，当天敌数量不足时种群可能迅速增加。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Corn | Aphids] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for clusters of soft-bodied aphids, sticky honeydew, sooty mold, leaf yellowing, and large colonies around tassels or upper leaves. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[玉米 | 蚜虫] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：可见成群软体蚜虫、粘性蜜露、煤污、叶片黄化，以及雄穗或上部叶片周围的大量虫群。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Corn | Aphids] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Scout colonies and natural-enemy activity before treating, conserve predators and parasitoids, and use registered controls only if aphid pressure is high enough to threaten yield. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[玉米 | 蚜虫] 问：如何防治？
答：施药前先评估蚜虫种群和天敌活动，保护捕食者和寄生蜂；只有虫口密度高到可能影响产量时才考虑登记防治措施。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Corn | Aphids] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Corn health depends on hybrid resistance, residue and rotation management, balanced fertility, and regular scouting from vegetative growth through grain fill because foliar diseases and insects can reduce photosynthetic leaf area. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[玉米 | 蚜虫] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：玉米健康与品种抗性、残体和轮作管理、均衡施肥以及从营养生长期到灌浆期的持续巡查密切相关，因为叶部病害和害虫会减少有效光合叶面积。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。
