## Chinese Cabbage - Downy Mildew / 白菜 - 霜霉病

[Chinese Cabbage | Downy Mildew] Q: What causes this?
A: Downy mildew in Chinese cabbage is caused by a water-mold pathogen (Peronospora) that is favored by cool, moist conditions typical of temperate growing seasons, though in tropical and subtropical regions it can occur year-round. Infection can happen as early as the seedling stage. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[白菜 | 霜霉病] 问：病因是什么？
答：白菜霜霉病由一种卵菌类病原（霜霉属）引起，该病原在温带生长季常见的凉爽潮湿条件下最为活跃，但在热带和亚热带地区则可能全年发生。感染最早可从幼苗期就开始发生。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Chinese Cabbage | Downy Mildew] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for pale, angular spots on the upper leaf surface, often followed by a grayish fuzzy growth on the underside during humid weather. Severe infection commonly occurs later in the season during the heading stage and can cause substantial yield loss if left unmanaged. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[白菜 | 霜霉病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：留意叶片正面出现的淡色角斑，潮湿天气下叶背常伴有灰色霉状物。严重感染通常发生在生长后期的结球阶段，若不加以防治可能造成显著减产。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Chinese Cabbage | Downy Mildew] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Apply protective fungicides preventively before symptoms appear in high-risk cool, wet periods, since the disease spreads fastest once established. Note that plants weakened by downy mildew become more susceptible to bacterial soft rot afterward, so managing downy mildew early also helps prevent a secondary soft rot outbreak. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[白菜 | 霜霉病] 问：如何防治？
答：在凉爽潮湿的高风险时段，应在症状出现前预防性喷施杀菌剂，因为该病一旦发生便传播迅速。需要注意的是，感染霜霉病后植株会更容易继发软腐病，因此及早防治霜霉病也有助于预防后续的软腐病爆发。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Chinese Cabbage | Downy Mildew] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Chinese cabbage is a cool-season leafy vegetable, and downy mildew, bacterial soft rot, and turnip mosaic virus together are considered the three most serious diseases affecting it across Asia and other growing regions. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[白菜 | 霜霉病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：白菜是一种喜凉的叶菜类蔬菜，霜霉病、细菌性软腐病和芜菁花叶病毒被认为是亚洲及其他种植地区危害白菜最严重的三大病害。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Chinese Cabbage - Bacterial Disease / 白菜 - 细菌性

[Chinese Cabbage | Bacterial Disease] Q: What causes this?
A: Bacterial soft rot in Chinese cabbage is caused by Pectobacterium bacteria, which produce enzymes that break down the pectin holding plant cell walls together. Plants that have already been weakened by downy mildew infection are known to become more susceptible to soft rot afterward. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[白菜 | 细菌性] 问：病因是什么？
答：白菜细菌性软腐病由果胶杆菌（Pectobacterium）引起，该细菌能产生分解果胶质的酶，破坏维持植物细胞壁结构的果胶。已知感染过霜霉病而变弱的植株，之后更容易感染软腐病。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Chinese Cabbage | Bacterial Disease] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Infected tissue becomes rotted, watery, and slimy, often with a distinctive unpleasant odor as the tissue breaks down. This is different from fungal leaf spots, which stay dry and defined; soft rot tissue collapses and turns mushy. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[白菜 | 细菌性] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：感病组织会变得腐烂、水渍化并呈粘滑状，组织分解时常伴有明显的异味。这与真菌性叶斑病不同，后者病斑保持干燥且边界清晰；而软腐病组织则会塌陷并变得糊状。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Chinese Cabbage | Bacterial Disease] Q: How can I manage it?
A: There is no chemical cure once soft rot sets in, so prevention matters most: manage downy mildew early since it predisposes plants to soft rot, avoid mechanical wounding of plants during handling since bacteria enter through wounds, and remove infected heads promptly to limit spread in storage and transit. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[白菜 | 细菌性] 问：如何防治？
答：软腐病一旦发生便无化学治愈方法，因此预防最为关键：及早防治霜霉病，因为它会使植株更易感染软腐病；操作时避免造成机械损伤，因为细菌通常从伤口侵入；及时清除感病的白菜球，以限制其在贮藏和运输过程中的传播。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Chinese Cabbage | Bacterial Disease] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Chinese cabbage soft rot is a particular concern during transit and storage after harvest, not just in the field, since the bacteria can continue rotting harvested heads if conditions are warm and wet. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[白菜 | 细菌性] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：白菜软腐病不仅是田间生产中的问题，在采收后的运输和贮藏阶段同样值得高度关注，因为在温暖潮湿的条件下，细菌仍会继续腐蚀已采收的白菜球。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Chinese Cabbage - Black Rot / 白菜 - 黑腐病

[Chinese Cabbage | Black Rot] Q: What causes this?
A: Black rot is caused by Xanthomonas campestris pv. campestris, a bacterium that can spread in contaminated seed, transplants, splashing water, and infected crop debris. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[白菜 | 黑腐病] 问：病因是什么？
答：白菜黑腐病由 Xanthomonas campestris pv. campestris 引起，可通过带菌种子、种苗、溅水和病残体传播。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Chinese Cabbage | Black Rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Yellow V-shaped lesions develop from leaf margins toward the midrib, veins may turn dark, and severe infection can cause extensive yellowing, wilting, and head rot. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[白菜 | 黑腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：叶缘出现向主脉延伸的黄色V形病斑，叶脉可变暗，严重时发生大面积黄化、萎蔫甚至结球腐烂。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Chinese Cabbage | Black Rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use disease-free seed and transplants, rotate away from brassicas, avoid overhead irrigation and working wet plants, remove infected residue, and sanitize tools and equipment. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[白菜 | 黑腐病] 问：如何防治？
答：使用无病种子和健康种苗，与非十字花科作物轮作，避免喷灌和植株潮湿时操作，清除病残体并清洁消毒工具设备。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Chinese Cabbage | Black Rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Chinese cabbage is a cool-season brassica. Clean transplants, good drainage, crop rotation, and frequent inspection of leaves and roots are important because bacterial, fungal, viral, and caterpillar problems can develop rapidly. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[白菜 | 黑腐病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：白菜是喜凉的十字花科作物。使用健康种苗、保持良好排水、合理轮作并经常检查叶片和根系十分重要，因为细菌、真菌、病毒和鳞翅目幼虫问题都可能迅速发展。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Chinese Cabbage - Clubroot / 白菜 - 根肿病

[Chinese Cabbage | Clubroot] Q: What causes this?
A: Clubroot is caused by the soilborne protist Plasmodiophora brassicae. Wet acidic soils favor infection, and long-lived resting spores can persist in infested soil. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[白菜 | 根肿病] 问：病因是什么？
答：白菜根肿病由土传原生生物 Plasmodiophora brassicae 引起，潮湿酸性土壤有利于侵染，其休眠孢子可在受污染土壤中长期存活。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Chinese Cabbage | Clubroot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Plants become stunted, pale, and wilt during hot periods, while roots develop swollen spindle-shaped or irregular galls. Young plants can die and older plants may fail to form normal heads. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[白菜 | 根肿病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：植株矮化、叶色变浅，并在高温时萎蔫；根部形成纺锤状或不规则肿瘤。幼株可能死亡，老株可能无法正常结球。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Chinese Cabbage | Clubroot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Avoid moving contaminated soil, improve drainage, use resistant varieties where available, rotate for long periods with non-brassica crops, and raise acidic soil pH according to soil-test guidance. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[白菜 | 根肿病] 问：如何防治？
答：避免携带污染土壤，改善排水，尽量使用抗病品种，与非十字花科作物进行长期轮作，并根据土壤检测结果适当提高酸性土壤pH。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Chinese Cabbage | Clubroot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Chinese cabbage is a cool-season brassica. Clean transplants, good drainage, crop rotation, and frequent inspection of leaves and roots are important because bacterial, fungal, viral, and caterpillar problems can develop rapidly. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[白菜 | 根肿病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：白菜是喜凉的十字花科作物。使用健康种苗、保持良好排水、合理轮作并经常检查叶片和根系十分重要，因为细菌、真菌、病毒和鳞翅目幼虫问题都可能迅速发展。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Chinese Cabbage - Aphids / 白菜 - 蚜虫

[Chinese Cabbage | Aphids] Q: What causes this?
A: Cabbage, turnip, and green peach aphids can colonize Chinese cabbage leaves and heads. Their sap feeding and rapid reproduction can distort growth and contaminate harvested heads. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[白菜 | 蚜虫] 问：病因是什么？
答：菜蚜、萝卜蚜和桃蚜等可聚集在白菜叶片和叶球上刺吸汁液，快速繁殖会导致生长畸形并污染收获产品。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Chinese Cabbage | Aphids] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Leaves become curled or yellow, plants may be stunted, and colonies of small soft-bodied aphids can cluster on leaf undersides or deep within developing heads. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[白菜 | 蚜虫] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：叶片卷曲或黄化，植株可能矮化，叶背或正在形成的叶球内部可见成群软体蚜虫。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Chinese Cabbage | Aphids] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Inspect inner and outer leaves, conserve natural enemies, remove heavily infested residues, manage weed hosts, and use selective registered controls when colonies are increasing. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[白菜 | 蚜虫] 问：如何防治？
答：检查内外叶片，保护天敌，清除重度受害残体并管理杂草寄主；虫群持续增长时再采用选择性登记防治措施。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Chinese Cabbage | Aphids] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Chinese cabbage is a cool-season brassica. Clean transplants, good drainage, crop rotation, and frequent inspection of leaves and roots are important because bacterial, fungal, viral, and caterpillar problems can develop rapidly. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[白菜 | 蚜虫] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：白菜是喜凉的十字花科作物。使用健康种苗、保持良好排水、合理轮作并经常检查叶片和根系十分重要，因为细菌、真菌、病毒和鳞翅目幼虫问题都可能迅速发展。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Chinese Cabbage - Diamondback Moth / 白菜 - 小菜蛾

[Chinese Cabbage | Diamondback Moth] Q: What causes this?
A: Diamondback moth damage is caused by Plutella xylostella larvae feeding on brassica leaves. Multiple generations can occur rapidly in warm conditions. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[白菜 | 小菜蛾] 问：病因是什么？
答：小菜蛾由 Plutella xylostella 幼虫取食十字花科叶片造成危害，温暖条件下可迅速发生多代。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Chinese Cabbage | Diamondback Moth] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Small green larvae scrape leaf tissue, creating windowpane patches and irregular holes. Heavy feeding can skeletonize leaves or contaminate heads with larvae and frass. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[白菜 | 小菜蛾] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：小型绿色幼虫刮食叶肉，形成窗格状斑和不规则孔洞；严重时叶片可被啃成网状，并在叶球中留下幼虫和虫粪。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Chinese Cabbage | Diamondback Moth] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Scout leaves frequently, conserve parasitoids and other natural enemies, rotate insecticide modes of action where treatment is required, and remove crop residues that support carryover populations. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[白菜 | 小菜蛾] 问：如何防治？
答：经常检查叶片，保护寄生蜂等天敌；需要施药时轮换不同作用机制，并清除可维持虫源的作物残体。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Chinese Cabbage | Diamondback Moth] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Chinese cabbage is a cool-season brassica. Clean transplants, good drainage, crop rotation, and frequent inspection of leaves and roots are important because bacterial, fungal, viral, and caterpillar problems can develop rapidly. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[白菜 | 小菜蛾] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：白菜是喜凉的十字花科作物。使用健康种苗、保持良好排水、合理轮作并经常检查叶片和根系十分重要，因为细菌、真菌、病毒和鳞翅目幼虫问题都可能迅速发展。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Chinese Cabbage - Turnip Mosaic Virus / 白菜 - 芜菁花叶病毒病

[Chinese Cabbage | Turnip Mosaic Virus] Q: What causes this?
A: Turnip mosaic virus infects many brassicas and is transmitted mainly by aphids in a nonpersistent manner. Infected weeds and volunteer brassicas can act as reservoirs. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[白菜 | 芜菁花叶病毒病] 问：病因是什么？
答：芜菁花叶病毒可侵染多种十字花科作物，主要由蚜虫以非持久性方式传播，感病杂草和自生十字花科植株可作为病毒库。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Chinese Cabbage | Turnip Mosaic Virus] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Leaves may develop mosaic mottling, yellowing, distortion, vein clearing, or ring patterns, and infected plants can become stunted with poor head development. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[白菜 | 芜菁花叶病毒病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：叶片可出现花叶斑驳、黄化、畸形、叶脉透明或环状纹，感病植株可能矮化并结球不良。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Chinese Cabbage | Turnip Mosaic Virus] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use healthy transplants, remove infected plants and brassica weeds, reduce nearby virus reservoirs, and manage aphid pressure early, recognizing that insecticides may not fully prevent rapid virus transmission. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[白菜 | 芜菁花叶病毒病] 问：如何防治？
答：使用健康种苗，清除病株和十字花科杂草，减少周边病毒来源并及早管理蚜虫，但要认识到杀虫剂并不能完全阻止蚜虫快速传毒。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Chinese Cabbage | Turnip Mosaic Virus] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Chinese cabbage is a cool-season brassica. Clean transplants, good drainage, crop rotation, and frequent inspection of leaves and roots are important because bacterial, fungal, viral, and caterpillar problems can develop rapidly. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[白菜 | 芜菁花叶病毒病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：白菜是喜凉的十字花科作物。使用健康种苗、保持良好排水、合理轮作并经常检查叶片和根系十分重要，因为细菌、真菌、病毒和鳞翅目幼虫问题都可能迅速发展。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

# CITRUS / 柑橘
# ===================================================================

## Citrus - Mites / 柑橘 - 螨

[Citrus | Mites] Q: What causes this?
A: Citrus rust mite (also called silver mite on lemons) and citrus broad mite are the main mite pests of citrus, thriving in warm, humid conditions. These mites are microscopic, requiring at least 10-20x magnification to see, so infestations are usually noticed only after fruit damage appears. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[柑橘 | 螨] 问：病因是什么？
答：柑橘锈壁虱（在柠檬上也称银虱）和柑橘阔恙螨是柑橘上主要的螨类害虫，在温暖潮湿的条件下大量繁殖。这些螨虫极其微小，需要至少10到20倍放大镜才能看清，因此侵染通常要到果实出现受害症状后才被察觉。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Citrus | Mites] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Rust mite feeding turns the fruit surface silvery on lemons, rust-brown on ripe oranges, or blackish on green oranges, an effect often called russeting or bronzing. Broad mite damage instead causes young leaves to curl and can leave scarred or cracked rind, mostly on the inner-canopy side of young fruit. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[柑橘 | 螨] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：锈壁虱取食会使果皮表面呈现银色（柠檬）、锈褐色（成熟橙）或黑色（未成熟橙），这种现象常被称为"锈斑"或"古铜化"。阔恙螨的为害则表现为幼叶卷曲，并可能在幼果内膛一侧的果皮上留下疤痕或裂纹。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Citrus | Mites] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Monitor from early spring through summer since this is when most damage occurs, checking young foliage early in the season and shifting to fruit inspection as the season progresses. Avoid unnecessary pesticide sprays, since they can kill the predatory mites that naturally keep rust mite and broad mite populations in check. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[柑橘 | 螨] 问：如何防治？
答：从早春到夏季持续监测，因为这是主要受害期，生长季早期应重点检查嫩叶，随季节推进转为检查果实。应避免不必要的农药喷施，因为这会杀死天然抑制锈壁虱和阔恙螨种群的捕食性螨类天敌。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Citrus | Mites] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Citrus rust mite damage affects only the rind's appearance and does not harm the fruit's internal quality, so growers weigh cosmetic market standards against the risk of disrupting biological mite control when deciding whether treatment is worthwhile. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[柑橘 | 螨] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：柑橘锈壁虱造成的损害只影响果皮外观，并不损害果实的内在品质，因此种植者在决定是否需要施药防治时，往往需要在市场对外观的要求与破坏生物防治平衡的风险之间进行权衡。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Citrus - Aphids / 柑橘 - 蚜虫

[Citrus | Aphids] Q: What causes this?
A: Several aphid species feed on tender citrus flush. Colonies develop on young shoots, and some aphids can transmit citrus viruses such as tristeza. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[柑橘 | 蚜虫] 问：病因是什么？
答：多种蚜虫可取食柑橘嫩梢，并在新生枝叶上形成虫群；部分蚜虫还可传播柑橘衰退病等病毒。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Citrus | Aphids] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Young leaves and shoots curl or distort, aphid colonies cluster on tender growth, and honeydew can lead to black sooty mold. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[柑橘 | 蚜虫] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：嫩叶和嫩梢卷曲或畸形，嫩梢上可见蚜虫群，蜜露还会诱发黑色煤污。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Citrus | Aphids] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Monitor new flush, conserve predators and parasitoids, manage ants that protect aphids, and avoid unnecessary broad-spectrum sprays when natural enemies are controlling colonies. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[柑橘 | 蚜虫] 问：如何防治？
答：重点检查嫩梢，保护捕食者和寄生蜂，管理会保护蚜虫的蚂蚁；当天敌能够压制虫群时避免不必要的广谱喷药。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Citrus | Aphids] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Citrus is a perennial fruit crop, so long-term tree health depends on irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, healthy roots, and repeated monitoring of new flush, leaves, twigs, and fruit for pests and disease symptoms. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[柑橘 | 蚜虫] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：柑橘是多年生果树，因此长期树势依赖合理灌溉和排水、均衡营养、健康根系，以及持续检查嫩梢、叶片、枝条和果实上的病虫害症状。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Citrus - Whiteflies / 柑橘 - 白粉虱

[Citrus | Whiteflies] Q: What causes this?
A: Citrus whiteflies and related species feed on leaf undersides and produce honeydew. Warm conditions can support repeated generations. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[柑橘 | 白粉虱] 问：病因是什么？
答：柑橘白粉虱等种类在叶片背面刺吸汁液并分泌蜜露，温暖条件下可连续繁殖多代。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Citrus | Whiteflies] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Adults and scale-like immature stages occur beneath leaves; heavy infestations produce honeydew, sooty mold, yellowing, and reduced vigor. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[柑橘 | 白粉虱] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：叶背可见成虫和鳞片状若虫，严重时产生蜜露、煤污、叶片黄化和树势下降。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Citrus | Whiteflies] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Inspect leaf undersides, conserve parasitoids and predators, control ants where they disrupt biological control, prune heavily infested shoots if appropriate, and use selective treatments when needed. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[柑橘 | 白粉虱] 问：如何防治？
答：检查叶片背面，保护寄生蜂和捕食性天敌；蚂蚁干扰生物防治时应加以管理，必要时适当修剪重度受害嫩梢，并优先采用选择性防治。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Citrus | Whiteflies] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Citrus is a perennial fruit crop, so long-term tree health depends on irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, healthy roots, and repeated monitoring of new flush, leaves, twigs, and fruit for pests and disease symptoms. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[柑橘 | 白粉虱] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：柑橘是多年生果树，因此长期树势依赖合理灌溉和排水、均衡营养、健康根系，以及持续检查嫩梢、叶片、枝条和果实上的病虫害症状。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Citrus - Thrips / 柑橘 - 蓟马

[Citrus | Thrips] Q: What causes this?
A: Citrus thrips feed on tender leaves and young fruit. Damage is greatest during periods of active flush and early fruit development. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[柑橘 | 蓟马] 问：病因是什么？
答：柑橘蓟马主要取食嫩叶和幼果，在嫩梢快速生长和果实早期发育阶段危害最明显。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Citrus | Thrips] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Young leaves may be distorted, while fruit develops silvery, corky, or ring-like scarring, often around the stem end where thrips feed while fruit is small. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[柑橘 | 蓟马] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：嫩叶可发生畸形，幼果表面出现银白色、木栓化或环状疤痕，常集中在果梗端附近。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Citrus | Thrips] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Monitor young flush and fruit after petal fall, conserve natural enemies, manage only when scarring risk is significant, and avoid unnecessary disruptive insecticides. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[柑橘 | 蓟马] 问：如何防治？
答：落花后重点监测嫩梢和幼果，保护天敌；只有疤痕危害风险较高时才进行防治，并避免不必要的破坏性杀虫剂。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Citrus | Thrips] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Citrus is a perennial fruit crop, so long-term tree health depends on irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, healthy roots, and repeated monitoring of new flush, leaves, twigs, and fruit for pests and disease symptoms. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[柑橘 | 蓟马] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：柑橘是多年生果树，因此长期树势依赖合理灌溉和排水、均衡营养、健康根系，以及持续检查嫩梢、叶片、枝条和果实上的病虫害症状。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Citrus - Citrus Leafminer / 柑橘 - 柑橘潜叶蛾

[Citrus | Citrus Leafminer] Q: What causes this?
A: Citrus leafminer larvae tunnel within young citrus leaves. Adult moths lay eggs on tender flush, and repeated flush cycles can support multiple generations. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[柑橘 | 柑橘潜叶蛾] 问：病因是什么？
答：柑橘潜叶蛾幼虫在嫩叶组织内潜食，成蛾将卵产在嫩梢上，连续抽梢可支持多代发生。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Citrus | Citrus Leafminer] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: New leaves show winding silvery mines, curled or distorted margins, and sometimes a dark line of frass inside the mine. Mature hardened leaves are rarely attacked. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[柑橘 | 柑橘潜叶蛾] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：新叶出现弯曲的银白色潜道，叶缘卷曲或畸形，潜道内有时可见深色虫粪线；老熟硬化叶片通常较少受害。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Citrus | Citrus Leafminer] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Focus management on protecting young trees and new flush, conserve parasitoids, avoid stimulating excessive flush with overfertilization, and use targeted controls only when young growth needs protection. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[柑橘 | 柑橘潜叶蛾] 问：如何防治？
答：重点保护幼树和新梢，保护寄生蜂等天敌，避免过量施肥刺激过多嫩梢；仅在需要保护新生叶片时采用针对性防治。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Citrus | Citrus Leafminer] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Citrus is a perennial fruit crop, so long-term tree health depends on irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, healthy roots, and repeated monitoring of new flush, leaves, twigs, and fruit for pests and disease symptoms. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[柑橘 | 柑橘潜叶蛾] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：柑橘是多年生果树，因此长期树势依赖合理灌溉和排水、均衡营养、健康根系，以及持续检查嫩梢、叶片、枝条和果实上的病虫害症状。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Citrus - Phytophthora Root Rot / 柑橘 - 疫霉根腐病

[Citrus | Phytophthora Root Rot] Q: What causes this?
A: Citrus Phytophthora root rot is caused by soilborne Phytophthora species. Saturated soils and contaminated irrigation water promote zoospore movement and root infection. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[柑橘 | 疫霉根腐病] 问：病因是什么？
答：柑橘疫霉根腐病由土传疫霉菌引起，土壤长期过湿和受污染灌溉水有利于游动孢子传播并侵染根系。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Citrus | Phytophthora Root Rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Trees decline slowly, leaves become pale green or yellow and may drop, feeder roots decay, growth weakens, and fruit production declines. Symptoms can resemble salt, flooding, or nematode injury. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[柑橘 | 疫霉根腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：树势缓慢衰退，叶片变浅绿或黄化并可能脱落，吸收根腐烂，生长减弱且产量下降；症状可能与盐害、涝害或线虫危害相似。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Citrus | Phytophthora Root Rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Provide good drainage, avoid overirrigation, use tolerant rootstocks when establishing orchards, keep irrigation water from spreading contaminated soil, and confirm uncertain cases before treatment. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[柑橘 | 疫霉根腐病] 问：如何防治？
答：保持良好排水，避免过度灌溉，新建果园时使用耐病砧木，防止灌溉水传播污染土壤，并在采取治疗措施前对不确定病例进行确认。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Citrus | Phytophthora Root Rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Citrus is a perennial fruit crop, so long-term tree health depends on irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, healthy roots, and repeated monitoring of new flush, leaves, twigs, and fruit for pests and disease symptoms. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[柑橘 | 疫霉根腐病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：柑橘是多年生果树，因此长期树势依赖合理灌溉和排水、均衡营养、健康根系，以及持续检查嫩梢、叶片、枝条和果实上的病虫害症状。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

# COWPEA / LONG BEAN / 豆角
# ===================================================================

## Cowpea - Thrips / 豆角 - 蓟马

[Cowpea | Thrips] Q: What causes this?
A: Thrips damage on cowpea is caused by species that feed directly on flowers and developing pods, most severe during dry periods when thrips populations build up fastest. Unlike on tomato or pepper, cowpea thrips damage is caused by direct feeding injury rather than primarily by the viruses they transmit. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[豆角 | 蓟马] 问：病因是什么？
答：豆角蓟马为害是由直接取食花朵和发育中豆荚的蓟马种类引起，在干燥时段最为严重，因为此时蓟马种群增长最快。与番茄、辣椒不同，豆角上的蓟马为害主要是直接取食造成的损伤，而非以传播病毒为主。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Cowpea | Thrips] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Leaves show mottling with a characteristic silvering appearance, later turning yellow and drying out. On pods, thrips feeding causes surface roughening, deformation, and a distinctive dark discoloration at the pod tips and heads, sometimes described as "black-heads and black-tails." Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[豆角 | 蓟马] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：叶片表现为斑驳并呈现特有的银白色光泽，随后变黄干枯。豆荚受害后表面粗糙、变形，荚尖和荚头出现特征性的暗色变色，有时被形容为"黑头黑尾"症状。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Cowpea | Thrips] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Hang blue or castor-oil-coated sticky traps to monitor and help suppress populations, and use mulch on the soil surface to disrupt the pupal stage of thrips, which develops in the soil before emerging as adults. Neem-based sprays are a lower-impact option where infestations are moderate. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[豆角 | 蓟马] 问：如何防治？
答：悬挂蓝色或涂有蓖麻油的粘虫板进行监测并辅助控制种群；在土壤表面覆盖地膜，以阻断蓟马蛹期的发育，因为其蛹期是在土壤中完成后才羽化为成虫。在虫害程度中等时，印楝素类喷剂是一种影响较小的防治选择。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Cowpea | Thrips] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cowpea is a legume grown for its edible pods and seeds, and because thrips feed directly on flowers and young pods, damage translates fairly directly into yield loss, making flowering-stage monitoring especially important for this crop. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[豆角 | 蓟马] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：豆角是一种以嫩荚和种子为食用部位的豆科作物，由于蓟马直接取食花朵和幼荚，其为害会较为直接地转化为产量损失，因此在开花期加强监测对该作物尤为重要。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Cowpea - Aphids / 豆角 - 蚜虫

[Cowpea | Aphids] Q: What causes this?
A: Cowpea aphids and other aphid species colonize young shoots, leaves, flowers, and pods. They remove plant sap and can also transmit several cowpea viruses. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[豆角 | 蚜虫] 问：病因是什么？
答：豆角蚜虫等种类可聚集在嫩梢、叶片、花和豆荚上刺吸汁液，并可能传播多种豆类病毒。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Cowpea | Aphids] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Look for dense colonies of dark or green aphids, curled leaves, sticky honeydew, sooty mold, weakened growth, and poor flowering or pod set when infestations are heavy. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[豆角 | 蚜虫] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：可见深色或绿色蚜虫密集群落、叶片卷曲、蜜露和煤污；严重时植株长势下降，开花和结荚受到影响。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Cowpea | Aphids] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Scout young growth, conserve predators and parasitoids, remove weed hosts, manage ants where needed, and use selective controls if colonies are expanding and damaging the crop. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[豆角 | 蚜虫] 问：如何防治？
答：检查嫩梢，保护捕食者和寄生蜂，清除杂草寄主，必要时管理蚂蚁；虫群持续扩增并造成危害时采用选择性防治。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Cowpea | Aphids] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cowpea is a warm-season legume grown for pods and seed. Flowering and pod development are especially sensitive to insect feeding, while clean seed, rotation, and good airflow help reduce seedborne and foliar diseases. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[豆角 | 蚜虫] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：豆角（豇豆）是喜温豆科作物，以嫩荚和种子为主要收获部位。开花和结荚期尤其容易受到害虫取食影响，而健康种子、合理轮作和良好通风有助于减少种传和叶部病害。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Cowpea - Mosaic Virus / 豆角 - 花叶病毒病

[Cowpea | Mosaic Virus] Q: What causes this?
A: Cowpea mosaic symptoms can be caused by several viruses, including cowpea severe mosaic virus and related viruses, transmitted by insects, seed, or mechanical means depending on the virus. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[豆角 | 花叶病毒病] 问：病因是什么？
答：豆角花叶症状可由多种病毒引起，包括豇豆严重花叶病毒等；不同病毒可通过昆虫、种子或机械接触传播。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Cowpea | Mosaic Virus] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Leaves develop light- and dark-green mosaic patterns, distortion, blistering, vein symptoms, and stunting; pod set and seed development may be reduced. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[豆角 | 花叶病毒病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：叶片出现深浅绿色花叶斑驳、畸形、水泡状隆起或叶脉异常，植株可能矮化，并影响结荚和种子发育。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Cowpea | Mosaic Virus] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use clean seed, remove symptomatic plants, manage weed and volunteer hosts, reduce insect-vector pressure, and sanitize tools where mechanical transmission is possible. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[豆角 | 花叶病毒病] 问：如何防治？
答：使用健康种子，清除有症状植株，管理杂草和自生寄主，降低传毒昆虫压力，并在可能机械传播时清洁消毒工具。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Cowpea | Mosaic Virus] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cowpea is a warm-season legume grown for pods and seed. Flowering and pod development are especially sensitive to insect feeding, while clean seed, rotation, and good airflow help reduce seedborne and foliar diseases. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[豆角 | 花叶病毒病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：豆角（豇豆）是喜温豆科作物，以嫩荚和种子为主要收获部位。开花和结荚期尤其容易受到害虫取食影响，而健康种子、合理轮作和良好通风有助于减少种传和叶部病害。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Cowpea - Anthracnose / 豆角 - 炭疽病

[Cowpea | Anthracnose] Q: What causes this?
A: Cowpea anthracnose is caused by Colletotrichum fungi and can be seedborne or survive in infected residue. Warm, humid, wet conditions favor infection and spread. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[豆角 | 炭疽病] 问：病因是什么？
答：豆角炭疽病由炭疽菌属真菌引起，可通过带菌种子传播或在病残体中存活，温暖高湿和多雨条件有利于侵染扩散。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Cowpea | Anthracnose] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Dark sunken lesions develop on stems, petioles, leaves, or pods, sometimes with pink or salmon-colored spore masses under humid conditions. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[豆角 | 炭疽病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：茎、叶柄、叶片或豆荚上出现暗色凹陷病斑，潮湿时病斑上有时形成粉红至橙红色孢子堆。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Cowpea | Anthracnose] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Plant clean seed, rotate away from susceptible legumes, remove infected residue, avoid working wet foliage, improve airflow, and use locally registered fungicides where disease pressure warrants. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[豆角 | 炭疽病] 问：如何防治？
答：使用健康种子，与感病豆科作物轮作，清除病残体，避免在叶片潮湿时田间操作，改善通风；病害压力较高时按当地建议使用登记杀菌剂。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Cowpea | Anthracnose] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cowpea is a warm-season legume grown for pods and seed. Flowering and pod development are especially sensitive to insect feeding, while clean seed, rotation, and good airflow help reduce seedborne and foliar diseases. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[豆角 | 炭疽病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：豆角（豇豆）是喜温豆科作物，以嫩荚和种子为主要收获部位。开花和结荚期尤其容易受到害虫取食影响，而健康种子、合理轮作和良好通风有助于减少种传和叶部病害。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Cowpea - Bacterial Blight / 豆角 - 细菌性疫病

[Cowpea | Bacterial Blight] Q: What causes this?
A: Cowpea bacterial blight is caused by Xanthomonas bacteria and is often introduced with contaminated seed. Splashing water and warm, humid weather promote spread. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[豆角 | 细菌性疫病] 问：病因是什么？
答：豆角细菌性疫病由黄单胞菌引起，常随带菌种子传入；溅水以及温暖高湿环境有利于传播。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Cowpea | Bacterial Blight] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: Leaves develop angular, vein-limited lesions with yellow halos that can enlarge into wedge-shaped blighted areas; severe infections may cause leaf drop, stem cankers, and lodging. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[豆角 | 细菌性疫病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：叶片出现受叶脉限制的角形病斑并带黄色晕圈，可扩大成楔形坏死区；严重时发生落叶、茎部溃疡和倒伏。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Cowpea | Bacterial Blight] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Use clean seed, rotate crops, avoid overhead irrigation and working wet plants, remove infected residue, and sanitize equipment that moves between fields. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[豆角 | 细菌性疫病] 问：如何防治？
答：使用健康种子，合理轮作，避免喷灌和植株潮湿时操作，清除病残体，并清洁消毒在田块之间移动的设备。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Cowpea | Bacterial Blight] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cowpea is a warm-season legume grown for pods and seed. Flowering and pod development are especially sensitive to insect feeding, while clean seed, rotation, and good airflow help reduce seedborne and foliar diseases. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[豆角 | 细菌性疫病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：豆角（豇豆）是喜温豆科作物，以嫩荚和种子为主要收获部位。开花和结荚期尤其容易受到害虫取食影响，而健康种子、合理轮作和良好通风有助于减少种传和叶部病害。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Cowpea - Powdery Mildew / 豆角 - 白粉病

[Cowpea | Powdery Mildew] Q: What causes this?
A: Cowpea powdery mildew is caused by powdery mildew fungi that spread by airborne spores and often become more visible during dry periods with moderate temperatures and humid nights. Risk is usually influenced by the presence of the causal pathogen or pest, a susceptible crop, and favorable environmental or field conditions. Monitoring recent weather, irrigation, nearby host plants, crop history, and pest activity can help explain why the problem appeared and whether it is likely to spread.

[豆角 | 白粉病] 问：病因是什么？
答：豆角白粉病由白粉菌类真菌引起，孢子可随空气传播，在白天较干燥、温度适中且夜间湿度较高时常更容易显现。 风险通常取决于病原或害虫是否存在、作物是否易感，以及环境和田间条件是否适宜。结合近期天气、灌溉方式、周边寄主、种植历史和害虫活动进行观察，有助于判断问题出现的原因及其继续扩散的可能性。

[Cowpea | Powdery Mildew] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: White powdery patches develop on leaves and may spread to stems or pods; severe disease causes yellowing, premature leaf drop, and reduced plant vigor. Check both upper and lower leaf surfaces as well as stems, flowers, fruit, and new growth where relevant. Note whether symptoms begin on older or younger tissue, whether they are scattered or spreading, and whether insects, webbing, spores, lesions, wilting, or distortion are present. Similar disorders can overlap visually, so several symptoms together are more reliable than a single sign.

[豆角 | 白粉病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：叶片出现白色粉状斑块，并可扩展到茎或豆荚；严重时叶片黄化、提前脱落，植株长势下降。 检查叶片正反面，并根据作物情况查看茎、花、果实和新生组织。记录症状先出现在老叶还是嫩叶、是零星出现还是持续扩展，以及是否伴有虫体、蛛网、孢子、病斑、萎蔫或畸形。多种特征同时出现通常比单一症状更有诊断价值。

[Cowpea | Powdery Mildew] Q: How can I manage it?
A: Improve spacing and airflow, remove badly affected material, avoid excessive nitrogen, use resistant varieties where available, and apply locally registered fungicides when disease pressure is significant. For integrated management, inspect plants regularly and act early before damage becomes widespread. Remove badly affected material when appropriate, reduce avoidable plant stress, keep tools and growing areas clean, and use irrigation, spacing, sanitation, rotation, resistant varieties, and natural enemies where they apply. If a pesticide is needed, use only a product registered for the crop and target problem in your location, follow the label, observe harvest intervals, and rotate modes of action when resistance is a concern.

[豆角 | 白粉病] 问：如何防治？
答：改善株距和通风，清除严重受害组织，避免氮肥过量，尽量使用抗病品种；病害压力较高时按当地建议使用登记杀菌剂。 综合防治应以定期巡查和早期处理为主。在适当情况下清除严重受害组织，减少植株胁迫，保持工具和种植区域清洁，并结合合理灌溉、株距、卫生管理、轮作、抗性品种和天敌保护。若确需使用农药，应仅使用当地登记用于该作物和目标病虫害的产品，严格遵守标签、采收安全间隔，并在存在抗药性风险时轮换不同作用机制。

[Cowpea | Powdery Mildew] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cowpea is a warm-season legume grown for pods and seed. Flowering and pod development are especially sensitive to insect feeding, while clean seed, rotation, and good airflow help reduce seedborne and foliar diseases. Good crop health also depends on regular scouting, appropriate irrigation and drainage, balanced nutrition, suitable spacing, weed control, and prompt removal of seriously affected plant material. Keeping simple records of symptoms, weather, irrigation, and treatments makes it easier to recognize recurring problems and evaluate whether management is working.

[豆角 | 白粉病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：豆角（豇豆）是喜温豆科作物，以嫩荚和种子为主要收获部位。开花和结荚期尤其容易受到害虫取食影响，而健康种子、合理轮作和良好通风有助于减少种传和叶部病害。 保持作物健康还需要定期巡查、合理灌溉和排水、均衡施肥、适当株距、杂草管理，以及及时处理严重受害植株。记录症状、天气、灌溉和防治措施，有助于识别反复出现的问题并判断管理措施是否有效。

## Andrographis - Leaf Spot / 穿心莲 - 叶斑病

[Andrographis | Leaf Spot] Q: What causes this?
A: Leaf Spot is the condition identified for Andrographis in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Its exact cause depends on the disease or pest involved; confirm the diagnosis from characteristic signs, field history, and local crop guidance before choosing a treatment.

[穿心莲 | 叶斑病] 问：病因是什么？
答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将穿心莲与叶斑病关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Andrographis | Leaf Spot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Andrographis with suspected Leaf Spot, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[穿心莲 | 叶斑病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑穿心莲发生叶斑病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Andrographis | Leaf Spot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Leaf Spot on Andrographis, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[穿心莲 | 叶斑病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似穿心莲叶斑病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Andrographis | Leaf Spot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Andrographis is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Leaf Spot.

[穿心莲 | 叶斑病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：穿心莲已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为叶斑病。

[苹果 | 红蜘蛛] 问：病因是什么？
答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将苹果与红蜘蛛关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Apple Tree | Mites] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Apple Tree with suspected Mites, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[苹果 | 红蜘蛛] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑苹果发生红蜘蛛时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Apple Tree | Mites] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Mites on Apple Tree, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[苹果 | 红蜘蛛] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似苹果红蜘蛛，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Apple Tree | Mites] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Apple Tree is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Mites.

[苹果 | 红蜘蛛] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：苹果已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为红蜘蛛。

答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将香蕉与根腐病关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Banana | Root Rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Banana with suspected Root Rot, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[香蕉 | 根腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑香蕉发生根腐病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Banana | Root Rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Root Rot on Banana, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[香蕉 | 根腐病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似香蕉根腐病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Banana | Root Rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Banana is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Root Rot.

[香蕉 | 根腐病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：香蕉已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为根腐病。

答：花椰菜已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为霜霉病。

## Cauliflower - Soft Rot / 花椰菜 - 软腐病

[Cauliflower | Soft Rot] Q: What causes this?
A: Soft Rot is the condition identified for Cauliflower in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Its exact cause depends on the disease or pest involved; confirm the diagnosis from characteristic signs, field history, and local crop guidance before choosing a treatment.

[花椰菜 | 软腐病] 问：病因是什么？
答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将花椰菜与软腐病关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Cauliflower | Soft Rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Cauliflower with suspected Soft Rot, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[花椰菜 | 软腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑花椰菜发生软腐病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Cauliflower | Soft Rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Soft Rot on Cauliflower, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[花椰菜 | 软腐病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似花椰菜软腐病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Cauliflower | Soft Rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cauliflower is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Soft Rot.

[花椰菜 | 软腐病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：花椰菜已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为软腐病。

[Chinese Cabbage | Leaf Beetle] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Chinese Cabbage is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Leaf Beetle.

[白菜 | 叶甲] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：白菜已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为叶甲。

## Chinese Cabbage - Soft Rot / 白菜 - 软腐病

[Chinese Cabbage | Soft Rot] Q: What causes this?
A: Soft Rot is the condition identified for Chinese Cabbage in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Its exact cause depends on the disease or pest involved; confirm the diagnosis from characteristic signs, field history, and local crop guidance before choosing a treatment.

[白菜 | 软腐病] 问：病因是什么？
答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将白菜与软腐病关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Chinese Cabbage | Soft Rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Chinese Cabbage with suspected Soft Rot, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[白菜 | 软腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑白菜发生软腐病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Chinese Cabbage | Soft Rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Soft Rot on Chinese Cabbage, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[白菜 | 软腐病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似白菜软腐病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Chinese Cabbage | Soft Rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Chinese Cabbage is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Soft Rot.

[白菜 | 软腐病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：白菜已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为软腐病。

A: For suspected Anthracnose on Citrus, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[柑橘 | 炭疽病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似柑橘炭疽病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Citrus | Anthracnose] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Citrus is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Anthracnose.

[柑橘 | 炭疽病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：柑橘已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为炭疽病。

答：怀疑玉米发生白粉病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Corn | Powdery Mildew] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Powdery Mildew on Corn, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[玉米 | 白粉病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似玉米白粉病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Corn | Powdery Mildew] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Corn is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Powdery Mildew.

[玉米 | 白粉病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：玉米已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为白粉病。

答：怀疑玉米发生缺锌生理病害时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Corn | Zinc Deficiency] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Zinc Deficiency on Corn, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[玉米 | 缺锌生理病害] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似玉米缺锌生理病害，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[黄瓜 | 黑星病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑黄瓜发生黑星病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Cucumber | Black Scab] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Black Scab on Cucumber, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[黄瓜 | 黑星病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似黄瓜黑星病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Cucumber | Black Scab] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Cucumber is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Black Scab.

[黄瓜 | 黑星病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：黄瓜已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为黑星病。
