[葡萄 | 黑痘病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑葡萄发生黑痘病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Grape | Black Pox] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Black Pox on Grape, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[葡萄 | 黑痘病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似葡萄黑痘病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Grape | Black Pox] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Grape is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Black Pox.

[葡萄 | 黑痘病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：葡萄已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为黑痘病。

答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将葡萄与煤污病关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Grape | Sooty Mold] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Grape with suspected Sooty Mold, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[葡萄 | 煤污病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑葡萄发生煤污病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Grape | Sooty Mold] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Sooty Mold on Grape, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[葡萄 | 煤污病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似葡萄煤污病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Grape | Sooty Mold] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Grape is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Sooty Mold.

[葡萄 | 煤污病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：葡萄已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为煤污病。

A: Lettuce is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Anthracnose.

[生菜 | 炭疽病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：生菜已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为炭疽病。

## Lettuce - Bacterial Disease / 生菜 - 细菌病

[Lettuce | Bacterial Disease] Q: What causes this?
A: Bacterial Disease is the condition identified for Lettuce in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Its exact cause depends on the disease or pest involved; confirm the diagnosis from characteristic signs, field history, and local crop guidance before choosing a treatment.

[生菜 | 细菌病] 问：病因是什么？
答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将生菜与细菌病关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Lettuce | Bacterial Disease] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Lettuce with suspected Bacterial Disease, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[生菜 | 细菌病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑生菜发生细菌病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Lettuce | Bacterial Disease] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Bacterial Disease on Lettuce, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[生菜 | 细菌病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似生菜细菌病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Lettuce | Bacterial Disease] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Lettuce is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Bacterial Disease.

[生菜 | 细菌病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：生菜已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为细菌病。

答：对疑似瓜栗叶尖叶缘枯焦，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Malabar Chestnut | Leaf Tip Burn] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Malabar Chestnut is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Leaf Tip Burn.

[瓜栗 | 叶尖叶缘枯焦] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：瓜栗已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为叶尖叶缘枯焦。

## Mango - Anthracnose / 芒果 - 炭疽病

[Mango | Anthracnose] Q: What causes this?
A: Anthracnose is the condition identified for Mango in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Its exact cause depends on the disease or pest involved; confirm the diagnosis from characteristic signs, field history, and local crop guidance before choosing a treatment.

[芒果 | 炭疽病] 问：病因是什么？
答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将芒果与炭疽病关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Mango | Anthracnose] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Mango with suspected Anthracnose, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[芒果 | 炭疽病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑芒果发生炭疽病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Mango | Anthracnose] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Anthracnose on Mango, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[芒果 | 炭疽病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似芒果炭疽病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Mango | Anthracnose] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Mango is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Anthracnose.

[芒果 | 炭疽病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：芒果已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为炭疽病。

答：怀疑发财树发生红蜘蛛时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Money Tree | Mites] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Mites on Money Tree, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[发财树 | 红蜘蛛] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似发财树红蜘蛛，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[绿豆 | 斑疹病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑绿豆发生斑疹病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Mung Bean | Spotted Wilt] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Spotted Wilt on Mung Bean, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[绿豆 | 斑疹病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似绿豆斑疹病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Mung Bean | Spotted Wilt] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Mung Bean is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Spotted Wilt.

[绿豆 | 斑疹病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：绿豆已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为斑疹病。

答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将豌豆与灰霉病关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Pea | Gray Mold] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Pea with suspected Gray Mold, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[豌豆 | 灰霉病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑豌豆发生灰霉病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Pea | Gray Mold] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Gray Mold on Pea, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[豌豆 | 灰霉病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似豌豆灰霉病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Pea | Gray Mold] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Pea is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Gray Mold.

[豌豆 | 灰霉病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：豌豆已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为灰霉病。

答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将桃与缩叶病关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Peach Tree | Peach Leaf Curl] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Peach Tree with suspected Peach Leaf Curl, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[桃 | 缩叶病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑桃发生缩叶病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Peach Tree | Peach Leaf Curl] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Peach Leaf Curl on Peach Tree, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[桃 | 缩叶病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似桃缩叶病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Peach Tree | Peach Leaf Curl] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Peach Tree is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Peach Leaf Curl.

[桃 | 缩叶病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：桃已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为缩叶病。

A: For Pear Tree with suspected Dry Rot, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[梨 | 干腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑梨发生干腐病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Pear Tree | Dry Rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Dry Rot on Pear Tree, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[梨 | 干腐病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似梨干腐病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Pear Tree | Dry Rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Pear Tree is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Dry Rot.

[梨 | 干腐病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：梨已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为干腐病。

答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将马铃薯与青枯病关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Potato | Bacterial Wilt] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Potato with suspected Bacterial Wilt, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[马铃薯 | 青枯病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑马铃薯发生青枯病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Potato | Bacterial Wilt] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Bacterial Wilt on Potato, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[马铃薯 | 青枯病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似马铃薯青枯病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Potato | Bacterial Wilt] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Potato is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Bacterial Wilt.

[马铃薯 | 青枯病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：马铃薯已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为青枯病。

A: Potato is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Viral Disease.

[马铃薯 | 病毒病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：马铃薯已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为病毒病。

## Pumpkin - Downy Mildew / 南瓜 - 霜霉病

[Pumpkin | Downy Mildew] Q: What causes this?
A: Downy Mildew is the condition identified for Pumpkin in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Its exact cause depends on the disease or pest involved; confirm the diagnosis from characteristic signs, field history, and local crop guidance before choosing a treatment.

[南瓜 | 霜霉病] 问：病因是什么？
答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将南瓜与霜霉病关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Pumpkin | Downy Mildew] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Pumpkin with suspected Downy Mildew, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[南瓜 | 霜霉病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑南瓜发生霜霉病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Pumpkin | Downy Mildew] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Downy Mildew on Pumpkin, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[南瓜 | 霜霉病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似南瓜霜霉病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Pumpkin | Downy Mildew] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Pumpkin is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Downy Mildew.

[南瓜 | 霜霉病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：南瓜已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为霜霉病。

[Red Bean | Mites] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Mites on Red Bean, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. [红豆 | 螨类危害] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似红豆螨类危害，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

答：怀疑水稻发生叶鞘腐败病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Rice | Leaf Sheath Rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Leaf Sheath Rot on Rice, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[水稻 | 叶鞘腐败病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似水稻叶鞘腐败病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

A: For Romaine Lettuce with suspected Soft Mold, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[油麦菜 | 软霉病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑油麦菜发生软霉病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Romaine Lettuce | Soft Mold] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Soft Mold on Romaine Lettuce, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[油麦菜 | 软霉病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似油麦菜软霉病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Romaine Lettuce | Soft Mold] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Romaine Lettuce is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Soft Mold.

[油麦菜 | 软霉病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：油麦菜已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为软霉病。

答：月季/玫瑰已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为煤污病。

## Rose - Stalk Rot / 月季/玫瑰 - 茎腐病

[Rose | Stalk Rot] Q: What causes this?
A: Stalk Rot is the condition identified for Rose in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Its exact cause depends on the disease or pest involved; confirm the diagnosis from characteristic signs, field history, and local crop guidance before choosing a treatment.

[月季/玫瑰 | 茎腐病] 问：病因是什么？
答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将月季/玫瑰与茎腐病关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Rose | Stalk Rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Rose with suspected Stalk Rot, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[月季/玫瑰 | 茎腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑月季/玫瑰发生茎腐病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Rose | Stalk Rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Stalk Rot on Rose, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[月季/玫瑰 | 茎腐病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似月季/玫瑰茎腐病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Rose | Stalk Rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Rose is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Stalk Rot.

[月季/玫瑰 | 茎腐病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：月季/玫瑰已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为茎腐病。

答：对疑似葡萄蓟马，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Shine Muscat | Thrips] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Shine Muscat is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Thrips.

[葡萄 | 蓟马] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：葡萄已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为蓟马。

## Soybean - Anthracnose / 黄豆 - 炭疽病

[Soybean | Anthracnose] Q: What causes this?
A: Anthracnose is the condition identified for Soybean in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Its exact cause depends on the disease or pest involved; confirm the diagnosis from characteristic signs, field history, and local crop guidance before choosing a treatment.

[黄豆 | 炭疽病] 问：病因是什么？
答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将黄豆与炭疽病关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Soybean | Anthracnose] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Soybean with suspected Anthracnose, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[黄豆 | 炭疽病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑黄豆发生炭疽病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Soybean | Anthracnose] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Anthracnose on Soybean, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[黄豆 | 炭疽病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似黄豆炭疽病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

A: Soybean is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Anthracnose.

[黄豆 | 炭疽病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。

答：怀疑菠菜发生痿螨时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Spinach | Eriophyid Mite] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Eriophyid Mite on Spinach, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[菠菜 | 痿螨] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似菠菜痿螨，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将草莓与茎基腐病关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Strawberry | Stem Base Rot] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Strawberry with suspected Stem Base Rot, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[草莓 | 茎基腐病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑草莓发生茎基腐病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Strawberry | Stem Base Rot] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Stem Base Rot on Strawberry, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[草莓 | 茎基腐病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似草莓茎基腐病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Strawberry | Stem Base Rot] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Strawberry is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Stem Base Rot.

[草莓 | 茎基腐病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：草莓已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为茎基腐病。

答：向日葵已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为菌核病。

## Sweet Pea - Powdery Mildew / 豌豆 - 白粉病

[Sweet Pea | Powdery Mildew] Q: What causes this?
A: Powdery Mildew is the condition identified for Sweet Pea in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Its exact cause depends on the disease or pest involved; confirm the diagnosis from characteristic signs, field history, and local crop guidance before choosing a treatment.

[豌豆 | 白粉病] 问：病因是什么？
答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将豌豆与白粉病关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Sweet Pea | Powdery Mildew] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Sweet Pea with suspected Powdery Mildew, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[豌豆 | 白粉病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑豌豆发生白粉病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Sweet Pea | Powdery Mildew] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Powdery Mildew on Sweet Pea, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[豌豆 | 白粉病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似豌豆白粉病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Sweet Pea | Powdery Mildew] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Sweet Pea is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Powdery Mildew.

[豌豆 | 白粉病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：豌豆已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为白粉病。

答：对疑似茶枯芽病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Tea | Bud Blight] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tea is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Bud Blight.

[茶 | 枯芽病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：茶已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为枯芽病。

## Tobacco - Bacterial Wilt / 烟草 - 青枯病

[Tobacco | Bacterial Wilt] Q: What causes this?
A: Bacterial Wilt is the condition identified for Tobacco in AgroMind's expanded label coverage. Its exact cause depends on the disease or pest involved; confirm the diagnosis from characteristic signs, field history, and local crop guidance before choosing a treatment.

[烟草 | 青枯病] 问：病因是什么？
答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将烟草与青枯病关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Tobacco | Bacterial Wilt] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Tobacco with suspected Bacterial Wilt, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[烟草 | 青枯病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑烟草发生青枯病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Tobacco | Bacterial Wilt] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Bacterial Wilt on Tobacco, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[烟草 | 青枯病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似烟草青枯病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Tobacco | Bacterial Wilt] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tobacco is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Bacterial Wilt.

[烟草 | 青枯病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：烟草已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为青枯病。

A: For suspected Canker on Fruit on Tomato, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[番茄 | 溃疡病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似番茄溃疡病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Tomato | Canker on Fruit] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Tomato is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Canker on Fruit.

[番茄 | 溃疡病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：番茄已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为溃疡病。

答：怀疑茭白发生纹枯病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Water Bamboo | Sheath Blight] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Sheath Blight on Water Bamboo, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[茭白 | 纹枯病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似茭白纹枯病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将小麦与赤霉病关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Wheat | Fusarium Head Blight] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Wheat with suspected Fusarium Head Blight, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[小麦 | 赤霉病] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑小麦发生赤霉病时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Wheat | Fusarium Head Blight] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Fusarium Head Blight on Wheat, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[小麦 | 赤霉病] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似小麦赤霉病，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Wheat | Fusarium Head Blight] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Wheat is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Fusarium Head Blight.

[小麦 | 赤霉病] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：小麦已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为赤霉病。

[枸杞 | 蚧壳虫] 问：病因是什么？
答：AgroMind扩展标签覆盖中将枸杞与蚧壳虫关联。具体病因取决于相应病害或虫害；采取防治措施前，应结合典型症状、田间历史和当地作物指导确认诊断。

[Wolfberry | Scale Insect] Q: What symptoms should I look for?
A: For Wolfberry with suspected Scale Insect, inspect affected leaves, stems, roots, flowers, and fruit for the pattern that led to this diagnosis, including discoloration, spots or lesions, distortion, wilting, rot, feeding damage, or abnormal growth where relevant. Compare several affected plants because a single visual symptom can have multiple causes.

[枸杞 | 蚧壳虫] 问：应该注意哪些症状？
答：怀疑枸杞发生蚧壳虫时，应检查叶、茎、根、花和果实，观察与该诊断相符的变色、斑点或病斑、畸形、萎蔫、腐烂、取食损伤或异常生长等表现。建议比较多株受害植株，因为单一视觉症状可能有多种原因。

[Wolfberry | Scale Insect] Q: How can I manage it?
A: For suspected Scale Insect on Wolfberry, isolate or remove heavily affected material when appropriate, keep tools and the growing area clean, avoid moving potentially infected plant material, and reduce avoidable plant stress. Use crop- and condition-specific biological, cultural, or chemical controls only after confirming the diagnosis and following locally registered product guidance.

[枸杞 | 蚧壳虫] 问：如何防治？
答：对疑似枸杞蚧壳虫，可在适当情况下隔离或清除严重受害组织，保持工具和种植环境清洁，避免转移可能带病的植物材料，并减少可避免的植株胁迫。确认诊断后，再按照当地登记和指导选择针对该作物和病害的生物、农业或化学防治措施。

[Wolfberry | Scale Insect] Q: Tell me more about this crop.
A: Wolfberry is included in AgroMind's expanded crop knowledge coverage. Good diagnosis combines visible symptoms with crop stage, recent weather and irrigation, pest presence, and how symptoms are distributed across the plant or field. For this record, the associated condition is Scale Insect.

[枸杞 | 蚧壳虫] 问：介绍一下这种作物。
答：枸杞已纳入AgroMind扩展作物知识覆盖。可靠诊断应综合可见症状、生育阶段、近期天气和灌溉、害虫出现情况以及症状在植株或田间的分布。本条记录关联的情况为蚧壳虫。
